(Gau).", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.29", "verse_number": 29, "en": "Ih'o (Panic). Upward, a written sign.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.30", "verse_number": 30, "en": "O'ag (Panic). Downward, a written sign.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "Alphabetical sounds, Panic and Chine.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "Gin, river. Woo, small lake. Long, large lake. Oak, house. Chan, city. Shan, country. F'da, the earth. Gwo, sun-light. Fung, wind. Dan, light, human understanding. Git'how, sun. Git, moon. Salock, stars. Fuche, the firmament.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "M'bow, horse. Gow, cow. Gan, man. Du'gan, woman. Ji, boy. Du'ji, girl. Aden, sky. G'shan, life. Sa, death. Da, ground. Shak, rock. Foam, wind. Soo, square. Inq or inqu, round.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "Gui or gu, street. Loo, road. Him, sweet. Soap, sour. Bai, sick. Eah, cure. Sam-shot, war. Du'ga, sword. Bek, weapon. Fox-ow, spear. So, dart, a stone, a sling. Em, to go before.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "Hid, interview. Hout, dissatisfied. Work, spinal. Bow'mi, Goddess of cattle. Son or songa, one who gives alms grudgingly. Shawn or shon, a stone-cutter. Shaw, a servant. Go'ta, a plowman. Chon'gum, flax. Hark, dark. Kin, roots, edibles. Hoe or hoa, pressure. M'how, priestess.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "Git'oo, light ahead, the way is clear. Wa'sha, darkness ahead, a melancholia. Seang, a river ford, a passage. Chin, a great ruler. Gone, pertaining to untruth, romance, fable. Gaup, a hostler, a lover of the turf; one who lives indecently with beasts. Show (o short), decline, falling or fallen. Gwo'oa, to weave, to make cloth, also cloth, a covering, a poet, one who clothes things. Min'bon, salutation, a prayer, supplication, especially standing. Kii, truth, a tribe called Kii, a religious tribe on the continent of Pan. An abused people are also Kii. Kii, learning, one who tries to understand. Gwom, sickness, a talisman. Laum, soil, a tiller, harvest, rich.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "Lun, sleep, trance, somnambula, intoxicated. Hoo, a marshal, leader, organized, system. M'hoa or m'hoo, to shout. Baw, preach, sing. Di'sa, young and therefore foolish. Wa'shu, a tribe of hunters, serpent slayers, persons who feed on raw flesh. M'shu, makers of stone weapons, also stone weapons with handles, axes. Ken'ong or ben'ang, young healer of the sick, the gift of spirits. Chawnt, to sing a song, a monotonous sound of not more than three notes, continued from sunrise to sunset without ceasing. A method of inducing the trance state. C'yu, a lawyer, an arbiter; one who fills the place of both lawyer and judge (a modern referee). Hook, to make fast, a bond sworn on the sacred wheel, Eoin. So'che, epidemic, a scourge, a judgment.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "Sai, faithful. Hon'yi, multitude, followers, rabble, fete of sacrifice. Sung'soup, miscellany, variety, a people who marry with strangers. Law, the feathered tribe, feathers. Shoe'ji, a gosling or young aquatic bird, a man who can not go to the hunt or to war without his mother; a shame-faced people. Shon, sharp, particularly witty, to cut right and left, evil prophecy. Bog'wi, a circus, an exhibition of trained animals, civilization, learning. M'wi, a tattling woman. Es, spirit, the unseen world. Es'fom, the wind that is good. Sa'fom, evil wind. Gut, a plague, a famine, black-rot, scabs, lepers.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "Emp'agatu said: I am the son of Se'moin, son of Jehovih. I am the change of voice of the Seen and Unseen. No man can measure my mysteries. I travel with the growth of mortals. Think not this is my all; it is not my all. I am like the consuming fire that never ceases to burn. I was with the ancients; am today and will be forever. I am he that receiveth new signs and symbols; the old I cast away as a worn cloth, and the new is my house of idols. Mortals are my working tools, and my records the food of all nations.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "Emp'agatu said: When my people are grown in the tongue; grown in the lips; grown in the throat; grown in the nose, then I come in a chariot of fire and consume their libraries. With a stealthy tread and silent whisper I say unto men: Go not back to the ancients, O my beloved, nor seal up your souls, that Eolin may enter and give you light. Know ye that words are but idols and graven images? But they will not hear, and the Es that is in them cries out with bondage, and I send the burning flame and burn their books into ashes, for I will deliver them from the bondage of the ancients.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "Jehovih, the mighty Eolin of old, saith: Go, My son, Emp'agatu, give to the swift-growing Es of mortals, larger signs and words. Behold, they grope in the ruins of the past for the idols of old.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "Emp'agatu said: By the angels in high heaven, the Sons of Jehovih, am I called forth in this day to yield up the records of the past. Think not that I yield up all. I yield up only to show the way of my labors. The learned men of Corpor shall also search me out of the earth and buried monuments, and my tablets shall read in the East, and West, and the North and South.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "Na'ah or na'ok, - house. Sho'ah. - rich country. Ba'a, - evil. Aman, - steadfast. Eden, - foundation. Sam'-Or, - the three sacred lights; thus, Sam three, Or, light.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "Le'bab, - heart. Re'gel, - foot. Be'ged, - clothing. Dad, - breast. Gad'ta'ah, - the hand showeth the way as this tablet showeth; i.e., this is my hand. Ekef, - palm of the hand.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "A'bad, - destruction. E'dam, - man. A'yin, - eye, and also to perceive. O'zen, - ear, to hear. Taf, - family. Resh, - head.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "Off, - the birds or feathered tribes. Le'bashed, - to be clothed. Toff, - timbrel; also psalmist. Mits'vah, - the enforced law, the established, the authority of judgment. Ay'in, - conceit, the hidden enemy in one's own heart; they were puffed up in their conceit and would not hear. Ar'bag'ebul, - the four quarters of the world (Kii).", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "Sef'fer, - scroll, book or tablet. Aven'ibi, - the belief that man was once a bird. The worship of certain birds because it was believed man sprang from them, hence Aven'ibi, the idolatry of birds; the philosophy that led to the idolatry of animals. Aheba, - the adorable love. Ya'sha, - upright. Alef, - foremost. Man or maun, - vessel.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "Ra'o, - the evil, the entity evil. As Ra, death, is to mortal things, so is Ra'o to spiritual. Mira, - a lamp. Mi'qut'so, - angular, corner. Kar'tugh'nuin, - magician; one with familiar spirits. Maqatteroth, - an utensil for burning incense. Nasag, - to ascend.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "Te'ke'ua, - supplication. Otsar, - storehouse against famine; also place for treasures. Ra'ka, - kingdom or king and his people, or father and his family. Aboda, - to labor or to make brick; also brick dried in the sun (ado'da). Zemar, - music, written notes of. Nat'sack, - chief musician.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "Asuk, - a pot. Yat'sar, - potter. Barzel or tubal, - iron. Os'ka'gar, - sun, moon and stars as a whole. Penak, - summer and winter. Gu'sa'touin, - out of all things comes some good.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "Lechen, - bread, or bread-basket, or food, or food-basket. Asen, - evil hand, or power to cast spell. Assogen, - evil foot, or power to walk around one's house and so cast evil on the house and family. E'o'ten, - the All Provider, the sign of offering. Edan, - prophecy. Hoe'dan, - angel visitation, or the manner of prophecy.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "Edam'ah, - country, or the earth or the lands. E'lo'ih, - Jehovih. Ba'ra, - the created world. Ofel, - darkness. Or, - light. Eo'sor, - organized, i.e., the light and darkness were created, and all things on the earth became organized.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "Master: Who art thou?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "Pupil: A Son of Light. Behold the sign and emblem.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "What sawest thou in the light?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "The altar of Eolin.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "What was the fashion thereof?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "The altar of incense and altar-fire rose up before me. The wind ascended and the stars shone in the firmament. A tree grew by the battlement and the black evil crossed the south-west. In the midst stood the cross of Eolin, studded with pearls and diamonds.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "What more sawest thou?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "In the south-east floor of the temple, satan, black with the smoke of blood and war, demanded my surrender. And he drew forth the flaming sword.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "What didst thou?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "I said, I pray to none but the Ever Present Creator. In Him I have faith. Thou I fear not.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "What next?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.12", "verse_number": 12, "en": "I came to the chamber of industry and I was taught a useful trade. After that I traveled north-west.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.13", "verse_number": 13, "en": "And was honored for thy good work and love of peace, I suppose?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.14", "verse_number": 14, "en": "Nay, Master. I was confronted by a crowned king. He bade me halt, and ordered me to pray to the God he served. I remonstrated, saying: I only pray to the Great Spirit, trusting only in good works done unto all men. Thereupon he flew into a rage, saying: Thou art the worst of men; thy soul shall feed the fires of hell. With that he hurled a javelin at me. 15. I escaped and traveled north, and came to a country most rich and prosperous, where many Israelites had gone before me.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.16", "verse_number": 16, "en": "Why didst thou not tarry there?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.17", "verse_number": 17, "en": "I did, for a season, but warriors came and possessed the land and drove the Israelites away.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.18", "verse_number": 18, "en": "What next?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.19", "verse_number": 19, "en": "I fled to the north-east, and came amongst savages, where I barely escaped being slain and feasted upon.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.20", "verse_number": 20, "en": "Which direction, then?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.21", "verse_number": 21, "en": "I traveled east and came into a country old in religion and philosophy. They had great riches for the rich and great poverty for the poor. Their philosophers wasted their time in reading the ancients.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.22", "verse_number": 22, "en": "Why didst thou not remain with them?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.23", "verse_number": 23, "en": "I was too poor to live with the rich, and too ambitious to live with the poor, who were little better than slaves.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.24", "verse_number": 24, "en": "Whither next didst thou travel?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.25", "verse_number": 25, "en": "Toward the north part of the middle kingdom, where I came amongst magicians and necromancers.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.26", "verse_number": 26, "en": "What of them?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.27", "verse_number": 27, "en": "They consulted the stars, and the moon, and the palms of their hands, and called up the spirits of the dead, who did appear before them. There was no industry amongst them, and I could find no employment with them. Neither did they assist one another.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.28", "verse_number": 28, "en": "Where next?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.29", "verse_number": 29, "en": "I went further south, where I came to an uninhabited country, the most favored under the sun. It was a place of joy and praise, filled with beautiful rivers, forests, plains and valleys, and countless singing-birds, all things raising up the ceaseless voice of glory to Great Eolin. Here I sat down and wept.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.30", "verse_number": 30, "en": "What, wept in so fair a place?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.31", "verse_number": 31, "en": "Alas, I remembered the crowded cities and warring empires. Here there were no people, and I could not live alone, so I traveled still further south.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.32", "verse_number": 32, "en": "And certainly found a good place next?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.33", "verse_number": 33, "en": "Alas, me. The country was good, the climate warm, and all things grew abundantly without labor.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.34", "verse_number": 34, "en": "And why not most excellent?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.35", "verse_number": 35, "en": "Voluptuousness was an ocean for them to bathe in. And for all sins, their priests taught them, that, if before they died, they called on Daeves, Son of the sun and Savior of men, they would ascend to the upper heavens on the third day after death. Not myself loving indolence nor lust, I departed out of that country.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.36", "verse_number": 36, "en": "Whither next?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.37", "verse_number": 37, "en": "Toward the south-east, coming into a land afflicted with priests, soldiers and beggars. So I fled further east.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.38", "verse_number": 38, "en": "And what then?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.39", "verse_number": 39, "en": "I came to a small settlement of Israelites where I was received by warm hands. Here I prepared to settle down in peace during all my days. But the state soon became attractive by its places of learning and the beauty of the gardens and glory of its manufactories. There being no idle people nor beggars amongst us, the idolaters of Hemah, Savior of men, accused us falsely and then declared war on us, and with a powerful army marched upon us, taking all our possessions. I escaped and turned westward once more.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.40", "verse_number": 40, "en": "Thy fate hath been hard. Why smilest thou?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.41", "verse_number": 41, "en": "Because, however hard hath been my fate, it is nothing to that which I saw had once befallen another people where I came next.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.42", "verse_number": 42, "en": "What of them?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.43", "verse_number": 43, "en": "This was a country once rich in ancient temples and monuments, but now ruined and desolate. Broken pyramids and colonnades, tumbling walls, and thorns and wolves, marked the once habitable places of mighty kings and high priests. By the tablets on the moldering walls I read that these people in ancient times long past were worshipers of idols and of Gods who professed to save the souls of men. And I saw that their pride and glory lay in ships of war and mighty weapons of death. Having myself learned the trade of a potter, I took up an ancient, ruined pot, and read this inscription on it: Because I am a Faithist in the Great Spirit, Eolin, I am enslaved by these idolaters. Alas, what is my crime?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.44", "verse_number": 44, "en": "Most pitiful place! Whence then?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.45", "verse_number": 45, "en": "I met a friend whose head had been compressed in infancy in order to make him a prophet. He took me into his private habitation and taught me how the brain and nerves of flesh could be changed in infancy by pressure to make the grown-up man of any character desired. Next he taught me the monotony of sound that brings on the prophetic spell and power to see the unseen. Thus did he expound the philosophy of miracles, even to dying and coming to life again.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.46", "verse_number": 46, "en": "Wonderful philosophy. Wilt thou show me some of these miracles?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.47", "verse_number": 47, "en": "I will, O Master, but the secret of their workings I can not show.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.48", "verse_number": 48, "en": "It is true, O friend! Surely, too, thou hast taught this wisdom to the world?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.49", "verse_number": 49, "en": "Nay; my teacher sent me south, to a school of prophets, where I learned the mysteries of invocation and prayer.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.50", "verse_number": 50, "en": "For what purpose hast thou visited my temple?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.51", "verse_number": 51, "en": "To make pots.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.52", "verse_number": 52, "en": "What, with all thy wisdom?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.53", "verse_number": 53, "en": "A useful employment is the highest service to the Maker of all.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.54", "verse_number": 54, "en": "Thou and thy people shall be my people; my harvests shall be thine; and my gardens and orchards; for He whose eye seeth all, is upon me, and I am His servant.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.55", "verse_number": 55, "en": "There are three more chambers in my temple: The first preserveth the wisdom of the ancients. The second is the chamber of industry and inventions.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.56", "verse_number": 56, "en": "In the third and last chamber are the secrets of the fullness of worship. The name of this chamber is Om, because it is here the recipients repose in spirit from all the cares of the earth. (Signs and pass-words, and form of initiation, withheld from publication, because the rites are still practiced.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "These, then, are the chief tribes, being some Faithists and some Idolaters, and these are such as were revered in after generations, to wit:", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "Hode, Si, Iz, Koo, Puit, Poit, Huit, Sem, Ham, Zerl, Haka, Shem, For, Gau, Park, Bah, Loo, Ong, Gam, Dan, Ine, Both, Asch, Howh, Bon, Art, Ia, Gaub, Don, Ref, Fet, Kii, King, Nu, Wis, Sin, Ox, Or, Ug, Wan, Ked, Mork, God, Suth, Eve, Lut, Rut, Kem, Josh, Pert, Sis, Yi, Haus, Kamp, Booh, Koa, Tu, Out, Dav, She, Ji, Ish, Fush, Ab, Ak, Gan, Loo, Mish, Woo, War, Hiss, Sout, Bir, Hush, Yiam, Duji, Gug (Ghard), Ke, Ail, Wang, M'hi, G'wan, He, Zoo, Sa, Lon, Gow, Fifi, Chine, Iaf, Jah, Ba, Goud, Goah, Fah, Ion, Yon, Sope, Ban, Jose, Bad, Dad, Abad, Adad, Joss, Sing, Fome, Du'e, Sam, Sar, Esk, Sham, Noah, Ives, Yi, Mu, Om, Dor, Frag, Kuk, Hum, Wok, Ise, Loke, Ia'a and Gad.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "Now am I strong in Thee, O Jehovih. Thy bow in the firmament encompasseth me on all sides. The follies of earth have turned mine eyes up toward thy holy place.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "I have beholden Thine enemies bowed down. The king and the mighty man of valor with the two-edged sword are hid away in a dark corner. There is no light for them. Sweet-scented flowers hast Thou grown up in the way of him who will not look upward; but a serpent cometh upon him and he crieth out against the vanities of the earth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "In the far-off corners of the world hast Thou sown the seed of evil and death, and the man of darkness hurrieth thither; but Thy glories he will not behold. He buildeth his foundation in perishable things. But the hope of the righteous man lighteth up the inner chamber. He beholdeth Thy judgment seat. He seeketh Thee in all good things and durable; he glorifieth Thee in Thy works.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "I will turn away from my house and my lands and look upward all my days. Yea, the fruits of the earth shall be my servants; my crown shall be woven by Thy hands.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "My heart pointeth up toward Thy kingdom; but the heart of the man of darkness is downward to things that perish? Thou hast set Eon (Aries or Te) in the midst, and he shall judge the living and the dead.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "Thou hast made me above the diamond and above all precious stones; but the man of darkness goeth after these. Thy children he careth not for; he seeth not. Verily, will I run quickly to them and lift them up. They shall be gems for my raiment; they shall become a glory in Thy sight. Yea, I will turn their eyes up toward Thy holy place.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "Before them will I set up Gau, and the magnitude and glory of Thy worlds shall entrance them.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "Thy unseen hand shall become seen, and my brothers and sisters shall read the diadems in Thy firmament. Thine hand hath quickened my eyes to see and mine ears to hear Thee, O Thou Life of my life!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "My understanding cometh out of Thy tablet (book). Its pages are graven with the sun, moon and stars. Yea, all things proclaim the words Thou hast written. None can counterfeit Thy handiwork. From Thy tablet will I read from the rising of the sun till the going down thereof.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "My hands shall be skilled with tools to do Thy labor. Fabrics will I weave, and my house shall be built within the square of Thy compass, for it shall be righteous work. My eye shall look toward Thy dwelling-place. I know Thy sight is upon me. I will be joyful before Thee, my Father!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "To none other will I bow myself in supplication and worship. Before Thee will I labor for peace on all occasions.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.12", "verse_number": 12, "en": "My hand will I uplift in Thy behalf, and yet no man shall feel the weight of mine arm upon him. By Thy crescent am I sworn unto Thee. By the seven stars and by the sun which is in the midst of the firmament and is the glory thereof, and by the moon, have I spoken.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.13", "verse_number": 13, "en": "Before Rea (Es, the air and ether), in Thy name have I sworn. I have lifted up my voice. I will have no diadem but that that is woven by Thee!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.14", "verse_number": 14, "en": "I will sing Thee new songs every day, and find new words of praise for Thy glories. My trials shall be as whet-stones and a hewing-ax. My talents shall increase in wisdom and in whatsoever way Thou leadest me for my own good.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.15", "verse_number": 15, "en": "Before Thy sacred altar will I come, knowing Thy angels will come also. Yea, they shall teach me thy decrees.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.16", "verse_number": 16, "en": "Thy gifts shall be my gifts, my portion, whatsoever seemeth just in Thy sight.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.17", "verse_number": 17, "en": "Thy messengers shall come to me, and my house shall be their house, they shall behold my labor; they shall admonish me wisely.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.18", "verse_number": 18, "en": "They shall teach me wisdom in all Thou desirest of me. The secret of my love to Thee will I tell them, and I know they will lift me up for Thy glory. In Thy praise will I sing forever, O Jehovih! Amen!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "Mithra said: Ye that invoke Me, the Swift, Shining, with sword uplifted. Invoke me on the Tablet of the sun, and the world, and the crown, high raised. Bow down low and say:", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "Behold, the rose, deep rooted in the earth. Jehovih riseth in majesty of All Light. His colors no man maketh, nor knoweth any man the cause. This subtle perfume, whence cometh it, and whither goeth it? What power fashioneth it, and propelleth it?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "O Thou, unseen Om! Is my spirit like perfume? Whence cometh it, and whither goeth it? Give me of Thy Mithra, O Om!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "Make my heart look upward to Thee, Thou all Pure, Heavenly! Wife, and Spouse and Mother! Thou that cometh, I adore Thee!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "The corporeal world hast Thou, O Jehovih, placed in the midst of the lowest places, bringing forth. Bind my soul not to it, Thou All Going-forth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "My heart no longer looketh downward; my corporeal passions have I subdued. Yea, even the diamond binds me not, the highest shining corporeal thing.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "Thou, O Corporeal Sun, burning and full of flame, that bringest forth the corporeal, growing harvests, hear me proclaim the All Eye that createth, making the souls of men. Thou, O Corporeal Sun, that makest of the things that are made, what a glory is thine! But far off, and nearer than Thou art, O Sun, is Jehovih, Who createth without things that are made, the All First Creator. He goeth forth, Jehovih. He cometh Om, the Bride and Mother, nursing with full breasts.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "O thou Moon, hear my upward soul aspiring above all corporeal things! Thou, my Love and Majestic and ruler of waters and winds, I adore Thee. But a greater, the All Great Om, fed thee. From her breasts wert Thou, O moon, high upraised. Eolin, the Mighty, touched Thee with his little finger and Thou hast sped on. Come Thou to me, O Om, spouse of the Seen and Unseen.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "O ye Stars, full of shining in the high heavens. Who can measure your far-off places? Who can see your great corporeal majesty? O what a glory is yours, thrown in the high heavens! Thither, far, the soul of my forefathers roam full of praise and prayer. Make me all pure, O Thou Om, that I may ascend.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "Send forth Thy Crown, O Eolin, Creator of all moving things. O Thou Incomprehensible and full of glory! Make me oblivious to all corporeal things, and fill me with Thy going-forth forever.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "Make me to look not backward nor to myself, but to behold Thee, O Jehovih, and Thy Lords and Thy Gods, strong in majesty and spirit high-soaring, forever! Amen!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "MASTER: Who art thou, and thy business?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "INITIATE: A weaver of fabric (or whatever labor he followeth) and lover of wisdom. I have come to learn of the wisdom of the Gods and Lords of heaven.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "For thy labor thou art honored by the Gods and Lord of heaven. But erst thou canst learn of heaven thou shalt be proved in corporeal parts. What are the elements?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "Earth, air and the other above the air.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "What is man and his destiny?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "Born of the earth, earthly; freed from the earth his inner part, the soul, ascendeth and dwelleth in the soul of things.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "What, shall a dead man live?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "Yea, and rejoice that he so liveth. I have witnessed the souls of men returned to the earth, reclothed in corporeal substance, and talked with them face to face in presence of witnesses.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "Then thou fearest not to die?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "I am in the keeping of Hong-she, Savior of man.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "Who was Hong-she?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.12", "verse_number": 12, "en": "The only begotten Son of the Unseen. He was the incarnate and spiritual Son of the All Light of heaven and earth, born of the Virgin Mi, who was descended from the far-off star, Tristya.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.13", "verse_number": 13, "en": "For what purpose came He? And whither hath He gone?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.14", "verse_number": 14, "en": "He was made by Great Eolin, Creator of all things, to take charge of this corporeal world, which is His, to keep it forever. He abideth still in spirit on this corporeal world.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.15", "verse_number": 15, "en": "Where and when lived He?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.16", "verse_number": 16, "en": "In the ancient days He lived in the far east.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.17", "verse_number": 17, "en": "What was His labor?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.18", "verse_number": 18, "en": "To do good. In Him was no evil; He was the All First who taught the power of the spirit over the corporeal part. All who have faith in Him, can also work miracles, nor shall such persons ever die.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.19", "verse_number": 19, "en": "What became of Him?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.20", "verse_number": 20, "en": "First He was bound on the wheel of Eolin; but the spirits of heaven unbound Him. Then His hands were thrust through with swords, but there was no wound. He was then bound on an altar of sacrifice and burnt, but His corporeal parts would not consume, and He walked forth unharmed. Then a sword was thrust through His body, but it made no wound and gave no pain. Then He was placed in a coffin and buried, but in three days he rose up out of the grave and went forth preaching.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.21", "verse_number": 21, "en": "What became of Him finally?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.22", "verse_number": 22, "en": "He still abideth on the corporeal earth, and at times taketh on a corporeal body and showeth Himself, He and His mother Mi, also.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.23", "verse_number": 23, "en": "What did He preach?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.24", "verse_number": 24, "en": "To do good unto others; to harm no man, woman, nor child, and to pray only to the Great Eolin; to heal the sick by sacred words, and by motions of the hand. It is to learn these sacred words and signs that I have come to this holy temple.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.25", "verse_number": 25, "en": "Before thou canst be entrusted with such signs and words, thou shalt be proven on thy God and Savior, that thy faith be manifest. Know, then, that although thou art blindfolded and seest not, yet thou art surrounded by Gods and Lords of heaven and earth, and by a mortal priest most holy and powerful. If thou hast faith in thine own words, and thus knowest the Son of the Sun, thou hast little to fear. Follow, therefore, thy attendants to the places of sacrifice, that we may bear testimony that thou art holy. Remember, also, that all men must be tried, either in the corporeal world or in the next; without this there is no resurrection for them.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.26", "verse_number": 26, "en": "I trust in Thee, O my Savior, Hong-she. Let the attendants lead me forth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.27", "verse_number": 27, "en": "M.: By virtue of my power received from Hong-she, Son of the All Light, Savior of men, I pronounce E'gau (i.e., he is Gau). Release him, O ye Lords of the Heavenly Host. As the All Holy Savior of men penetrateth the corporeal part, so shall the corporeal part be divided.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.28", "verse_number": 28, "en": "I.: In Thee, O my Savior, God of this corporeal world, put I my trust. Lead me forth, O ye of the corporeal earth, and prove my soul.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.29", "verse_number": 29, "en": "M.: By Thy Light, Hong-she, descended in Fo'e'tse, angel messenger of the corporeal world, this is my brother of Chine and legion of Zerl. But yet a greater gift hast Thou for him. Lead him forth, O ye Lords of the corporeal world, and thrust a sword through his body.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.30", "verse_number": 30, "en": "I.: Go forth, O ye Lords of the corporeal world, ye know not what manner the spirit is of. Forgive them, O Almighty, Hong-she, Son of Eolin, Creator of worlds. This is Thy body, this Thy blood. Let them eat and drink of them, in remembrance of Thee. Thy hand will deliver; my soul shall never die!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.31", "verse_number": 31, "en": "I.: In remembrance of Him do I these things. Behold the power of faith. By faith the All Creator created.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.32", "verse_number": 32, "en": "M.: By thy words shalt thou be proven. O ye Lords of the corporeal world, bring him sand and water and a veil. By His command shall a rose bloom in our midst.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.33", "verse_number": 33, "en": "I.: O Thou, Hong-she, only first begotten Son of Du'e Mi, Mother of Eolin, Spouse of the All Light, offspring of the Creator of worlds, give me of Thy power for the blessings of the corporeal world.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.34", "verse_number": 34, "en": "I.: As Gau from nothing sprang and stood triumphant on the corporeal world; so, forth, come thou, the fibre of corporeal parts, and clothe the spirit-rose mine eyes behold. (The rose or roses are then beholden, fresh grown amid the sand and water.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.35", "verse_number": 35, "en": "M.: This day Thee I crown my brother He'den'loo (Magi), of the Savior empowered on the corporeal earth to dwell by holy land and water!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.36", "verse_number": 36, "en": "I.: This is My blood and My Body, saith Hong-she, the All Quickener. Take ye of them in my name.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.37", "verse_number": 37, "en": "THE LORDS (together): Behold the Es that ruleth over Corpor. Be mighty in will, O children of men. Be wise of will, O children of men. Be all truth in will, O children of men. Be all good works, O children of men. In all your best thoughts and wise perceiving, O children of men, learn from Es, the world unseen.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.38", "verse_number": 38, "en": "(The Lords conduct him to the middle chamber, where he taketh the oath.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.39", "verse_number": 39, "en": "I.: To celibacy I am sworn, for he who begetteth a child is bound in spirit, after death, unto his own offspring.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.40", "verse_number": 40, "en": "To the Es world I am all remembrance. To the corporeal world all forgetful henceforth forever.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.41", "verse_number": 41, "en": "All vain words do I renounce; all idle laughter do I renounce; all love of corporeal things do I renounce with abhorrence.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.42", "verse_number": 42, "en": "M.: What of the Es, the great Unseen?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.43", "verse_number": 43, "en": "I.: Two heavens there are: one resteth on the corporeal earth; one standeth high in the firmament.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.44", "verse_number": 44, "en": "Betwixt the twain lieth the bridge Chinvat, where standeth Hong-she, Savior of men. By His love can the children of men pass; by His curse must they return to the lower heaven till purged of all corporeal thoughts.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.45", "verse_number": 45, "en": "By the trumpet, loud sounding, of Fo'e'tses, Chief of the Heavenly Host, knowest the Son of the All Light the secrets of the souls of men.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.46", "verse_number": 46, "en": "(The Gods (angels) stationed at the four gates now come forward and salute. Then come the Lords (angels) of the outer host, with the working tools, and, together, they sing to the Great He'jo'is.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.47", "verse_number": 47, "en": "M.: For what purpose is this coffin?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.48", "verse_number": 48, "en": "I.: That I may be coffined and buried in the corporeal earth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.49", "verse_number": 49, "en": "M.: Let the lords of earth bury him in the name of Anra'mainyus (or Ugh'sa), the all corporeal death.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.50", "verse_number": 50, "en": "The initiate is then coffined and buried, and a watch set around the grave, over which a veil is thrown, and in the darkness the angels unearth him and set him free. Thereupon he is bestowed with regalia and implements, and with signs and holy words, the which can not be given outside the Sun degree of Faithists. Neither can the implements and working tools of the Lords and Gods of the outer circle be revealed save to such as have been duly prepared by fasting and by prayer, and by a knowledge of the motions and positions of the corporeal worlds. Jehovih saith: Only to the wise, the pure, and the just, do I reveal the mysteries of My kingdoms.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "Master: What seest thou?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "Initiate: The world lieth before me. Yea, the wide earth and all its riches. The living things upon it, and in the waters, and every breathing thing, and pearls and diamonds, and gold and silver; and at my hand, the rose and the lily, adorned by the Hand of Elohim.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "M.: Seest thou nothing more?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "I.: Yea, Master, the canopy of the firmament of heaven. In the midst thereof the light, burning sun, propeller of the vegetable world; the glory of day, and maker of light.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "On either hand, in the vault of the firmament, countless stars, saying: In the glory of our magnitude, O man, forget not Him whose finger upraised the firmament!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "M.: What more beholdest thou?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "I.: The moon, who changeth her size and place according to the nightwatch of the Ghads who minister to the wants of mortals.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "M.: Is this all?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "I.: Nay, Master, on either side I behold a hand, one pointing upward and one downward.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "One side of the world is all light and one side all black, and the hand that pointeth upward is on the light side, and the hand that pointeth downward is on the dark side. Here lieth the brush that wrote, and the sacred Gau, and above them the symbol of the burning candles of the ancients. Above these I behold the ark of the prophecy.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "M.: Why haltest thou in thy speech? What seest thou?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.12", "verse_number": 12, "en": "I.: Alas, O Master, above the world I behold an evil foot, black and clothed with serpents.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.13", "verse_number": 13, "en": "Above the twin swords, crossed, is the sacred name, Elohim.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.14", "verse_number": 14, "en": "M.: What seest thou in the midst of the tablet, black and as with a net woven around?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.15", "verse_number": 15, "en": "I.: A new corporeal world, rich in growing things; sprung from the surface thereof, hang the sacred signs of holy Lords and Gods, appointed by the most high Elohim, apprised and guarding over the morning and evening of the first days.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.16", "verse_number": 16, "en": "Here the trumpet calleth to the low earth, and to the spirits of mortals now dead, to come forth, inspiring teachers in all useful labors, to the new world above.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.17", "verse_number": 17, "en": "Fabric woven in the firmament of heaven is descending to the infant a'su. Thirsting for a kingdom in the lower heaven, standeth Baugh-ghan-ghad, the tyrant of newborn spirits in the lower heavens, watching the new earth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.18", "verse_number": 18, "en": "His tablet is a coffin filled with serpents' tongues. In his footpath millions of dead that sang his name, to make him triumphant over Elohim, creep, accursing him in high heaven.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.19", "verse_number": 19, "en": "The pyramids, the temples of ancient Saviors; and the overflowing holy basin, and lighted Nu'ba, candlestick of Holy Sacrifice, and the black claw, the Anra'mainyus (the devil), are cast down and gone, yea, on the earth below another God is dead. But high up in the firmament, the seven sacred stars still shine, and the emblems graven by the Great Spirit.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.20", "verse_number": 20, "en": "M.: What are the working tools of man born of woman? What of the Gods and angels beside Elohim?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.21", "verse_number": 21, "en": "I.: Alas, O Master, I know not.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.22", "verse_number": 22, "en": "My friend, since thou art learned in Gau, why comest thou to learn the signs and ceremonies of the ancients?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.23", "verse_number": 23, "en": "That I may unite myself with other men, and thus become a greater power to do good unto mortals.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.24", "verse_number": 24, "en": "Thou art wise. Repair now before my proper officers for further examination, and, if thou art proficient, come again before me and I will conduct thee into the chamber of Ophra'or'jhi, and there initiate thee according to the rite of the ancients.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "Which way, man?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "To the tree of knowledge.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "I go thither, also.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "Where, then, is thy treasure?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "I bring this pitcher and pot, a tame gowt and a dead man's skull. I have no more.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "Seffas bows down in thy favor. Hast thou slain any living creature?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "Nay, Ong-a-pa, but housed in a mound high-built, and steep, I have slept my nights away in peace, slaying naught.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "Hast thou the Hagaw'sa (the head compressed), from infancy?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "On my breast, engraved, the sign single standeth most honored of all I have. When I was a child my head was thus compressed, to school the judgment down beneath the prophetic sight. I have seen the Es world and the angels of the dead.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "Hold up thine hand, and show me, too, the leaf sign of Egoquim. Then the burning flame of sacrifice and monstrous serpents thou wouldst rather feed than destroy?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "To engrave the sacred symbols in Corpor I was well-educated. Behold I write.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.12", "verse_number": 12, "en": "Thy skill is excellent. Go thou to the south-west corner, and in the ark of the black hand thou shalt find a charm for venomous serpents. Bring it hither.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.13", "verse_number": 13, "en": "Alas, O Onga, ask of me any service but to deal in charms against anything.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.14", "verse_number": 14, "en": "How sleepest thy father and thy mother, and thy wife and thy sons and daughters?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.15", "verse_number": 15, "en": "On the mounds and in the mounds, O Onga. Hid are we all away from the devouring hiss-sa (serpent), and baugh and mieuh (lions and tigers).", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.16", "verse_number": 16, "en": "How was it with thy forefathers and foremothers?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.17", "verse_number": 17, "en": "In the mounds and on the mounds, O Onga. For a thousand generations my ancestors killed not any living creature Egoquim had created.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.18", "verse_number": 18, "en": "What is yonder building in the north-east?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.19", "verse_number": 19, "en": "The temple of Egoquim.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.20", "verse_number": 20, "en": "What meaneth it?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.21", "verse_number": 21, "en": "It is the sacred house of Dan. (Faithists.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.22", "verse_number": 22, "en": "What are the signs?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.23", "verse_number": 23, "en": "The All Light hand teacheth mortals to ever reach upward; the cup, that all the firmament is filled by the presence of Egoquim.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.24", "verse_number": 24, "en": "What is the sign of half a dog, of half a horse, and a man's head?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.25", "verse_number": 25, "en": "That man at best is two beings, a beast and a spirit.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.26", "verse_number": 26, "en": "What is the sign of the black hand and black onk, the slaying tool?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.27", "verse_number": 27, "en": "The mortal who slayeth the wolf and serpent hath a hard time in heaven.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.28", "verse_number": 28, "en": "What is the hard time of the slaying-man in heaven?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.29", "verse_number": 29, "en": "In the firmament of heaven he is haunted; the spirits of the slain come upon him. He findeth no place. With his black hand of death uplifted he crieth out.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.30", "verse_number": 30, "en": "What is the sign of Oke-un (tent), and the sign of the black head of the Ghi-ee (eagle), and the black mouth with black tongue projecting?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.31", "verse_number": 31, "en": "That all the earth must be subdued by man. Even the blackness (slander) that issueth out of man's mouth must also be subdued.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.32", "verse_number": 32, "en": "What are the signs of the ear, the triangle and the square, the evil quarters, the wedge and the vessels of copper?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.33", "verse_number": 33, "en": "Alas, O Onga, I know not.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.34", "verse_number": 34, "en": "What are the signs of the trumpet, the windlass, the hewing-ax and the altar of fire?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.35", "verse_number": 35, "en": "Alas, O Onga, I know not.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.36", "verse_number": 36, "en": "What are the signs of the Az-aj (stretcher), and the basket?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.37", "verse_number": 37, "en": "Alas, O Onga, I know not.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.38", "verse_number": 38, "en": "What is the sign of the men ascending the Orugh (the stairs), and lamp and the trees and the black fish?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.39", "verse_number": 39, "en": "First, there was an egg, and then a fish, and then trees, then man, and he saw the light and the sun. Then wisdom came and he learnt of Egoquim, ever after.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.40", "verse_number": 40, "en": "Thou art wise, and now, by virtue of my power and wisdom and love, do I crown thee brother of the Hoanga (prophets). Peace be with thee, Amen!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.41", "verse_number": 41, "en": "He who taught thee all the other signs will now teach thee three thou knowest not. After which the Oi will invest thee with cloth and with the signs of Chaigi (words of enchantment), that when thou travelest in far-off lands thou shalt be received as a brother.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "MARS (alias Death): What is this noise and confusion? Who comes here to disturb our haven of rest?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "JUPITER (alias Aises, a leader): Hark! I heard a voice! It is true, this place is haunted! Say thou, spirit or angel, speak! Who art thou?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "M.: I am Death! All who enter here must die! Are ye prepared?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "MERCURY (alias Saug, heavy): Hark! What fearful words!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "M.: Silence!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "J.: Alas, O Death, spare us! Command us as thou wilt, but slay us not.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "M.: Impious mortals, know ye this place is consecrated to the spirits of the dead? All who enter here must die!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "Mer.: We implore Thee, O Death! Is there no respite?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "M.: Over your heads I hold the two-edged sword. Fear ye, and tremble when I command.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "J.: Alas, O Death, we and all mortals fear thee. What shall we do?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "M.: There is one respite. Sit ye at my feet in token of your submission to a higher power than mortals. (The initiates sit down.) Bide ye there till I call my fellow-God, Life. Ye shall know your doom! All hail, O Life! Hither, hither! Mortal intruders have profaned our sacred chamber! Come thou and deal with them, that I may receive their souls!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.12", "verse_number": 12, "en": "SUN (alias Dan, a light): Hail, O Death! I come! Mighty art thou, O Death! Were it not for thee mortals would esteem themselves Gods. Take me, brother, to them. (M. conducts S. to another part of the chamber.) S.: Mortals, for what purpose are ye here? Speak to me, I am Life.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.13", "verse_number": 13, "en": "Pity us, O Life! We are friends to the spirits of the dead. We have come hither to unite ourselves in a common brotherhood, especially to improve ourselves in spirit communion. Deal thou mercifully with us and we will be faithful and true.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.14", "verse_number": 14, "en": "S.: On one condition only can I save you from Death's hand. I have here scales on which I weigh mortals, and by your words shall ye be weighed, for all words manifest the spirit within. If, therefore, ye repeat after me the words I utter, ye shall live. But if ye do not so utter them, ye shall be handed over to Death. Neither will I utter aught but what all good men and true, may utter my words after me.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.15", "verse_number": 15, "en": "J.: Speak, O Life! We will repeat after thee.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.16", "verse_number": 16, "en": "MER.: We will repeat after thee.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.17", "verse_number": 17, "en": "VENUS (alias due'ji): We will utter thy words!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.18", "verse_number": 18, "en": "CERES (alias hyastra): Speak, O Life, thy words shall be our words.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.19", "verse_number": 19, "en": "S.: My friends, repeat your own names and then these words (the initiates give their names): Of my own free will, in presence of these spirits and mortals, do I covenant that I will forever keep and never reveal any of the secrets or mysteries I may receive within this chamber. That I will accept as my brethren all who are here present, and all who have been heretofore initiated, or may be hereafter initiated, as fully and entirely as if they were my own blood and kin. That I will not from this time forth utter one slanderous word against these my brethren, nor against any man nor woman nor child belonging to them or that is dependent on them. That I will protect their honor and virtue and love as sacredly as I would my own mother. That I will from this time forth engage myself in some useful employment, and from the excess of my wages, contribute to the relief and assistance of any of the brethren who may be helpless or distressed. So keep me, Thou, who makest and rulest over all. Make me steadfast to keep this, my solemn covenant; and I bind myself under no less a penalty than to forfeit all spirit presence both in this world and the next, and the good-fellowship of all men, if I should fail to keep this, my holy covenant. By the Pillars of the castle I swear, and by the Sun, and by Uz (Osiris), and by the Sacred Wheel, and by the Angle of Gau, yea, by Elohim!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.20", "verse_number": 20, "en": "S.: My friends, you have been weighed, and the scales bear to your favor. You shall live. What more desire ye!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.21", "verse_number": 21, "en": "ALL: Light! (The ceremony so far being in the dark.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.22", "verse_number": 22, "en": "S.: Let my servants make a light. (The servants march about, but make no light.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.23", "verse_number": 23, "en": "First Servant: Alas, O Master, we can not make a light; the oil will not burn; the lamps are cold.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.24", "verse_number": 24, "en": "S.: In this emergency, what shall be done?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.25", "verse_number": 25, "en": "SECOND SERVANT: Alas, O Master, we know not.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.26", "verse_number": 26, "en": "S.: Let this be a lesson to both spirits and mortals, for as I do now, so in all great trials, do ye also. (Aside:) Hither, O brother God! Hither, O my brother!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.27", "verse_number": 27, "en": "M.: Here, O Life! Here, O Life, I come!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.28", "verse_number": 28, "en": "S.: My attendants can not make a light. The oil will not burn. The lamps are cold. What shall be done?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.29", "verse_number": 29, "en": "M.: Let us supplicate Him who is greater than life and death!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.30", "verse_number": 30, "en": "S.: It is well.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.31", "verse_number": 31, "en": "M. and S. (together): O Thou, Almighty and Everlasting Creator of Life, and Master of Death, give us light! Give us light, O Father!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.32", "verse_number": 32, "en": "S.: Let my servants try once more. (The servants now make a light. The initiates behold their strange apparel, which was put on them in the dark.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.33", "verse_number": 33, "en": "M.: Behold, O mortals, by the light of heaven, man hath risen above the beasts of the field and fowls of the air. But for the light of Uz (Osiris) your heads would have this day been as your hoods. (With his sword he knocks off the head-covering.) Be as beasts no more, but men and women. In token of your fidelity to the covenant, drink ye of this nectar of life.
