(Because they were saved from death.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.34", "verse_number": 34, "en": "S.: (making the proper sign, which is withheld from publication, and taking the hand of one of the initiates): Arise, O my brother of the Haunted Chamber, I salute thee in the name of ____ (withheld from publication).", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.35", "verse_number": 35, "en": "M.: As ye are now raised up on earth, so shall ye be raised in heaven, where, it is represented by the spirits of the second heavens, are scales to weigh the spirits of mortals recently dead. And he who presideth over the scales in heaven was called _____ (alas me, how shall I repeat his name?) Let him who conducted you hither answer me: Who is the God of the Scales in heaven?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.36", "verse_number": 36, "en": "FOURTH SERVANT: I can not so answer, Master.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.37", "verse_number": 37, "en": "S.: Knowest thou?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.38", "verse_number": 38, "en": "FOURTH SERVANT: I am wise.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.39", "verse_number": 39, "en": "S.: What wilt thou?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.40", "verse_number": 40, "en": "FOURTH SERVANT: (Withheld from publication.) (And when Anubis appeareth, the Master saith): All words sprung from the fullness of the spirit within. Be exact in observing your covenant, and guarded in your words toward all men, for as ye were here weighed by your words, so shall ye be both in this world and the next.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.41", "verse_number": 41, "en": "M.: Thus endeth your initiation, and may the cross of the Wheel of the Great Spirit be the center of your action, and the angle of your behavior toward all men. Amen! (All respond, Amen!)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.42", "verse_number": 42, "en": "FIRST INITIATE: Why, this is just the brotherhood we desired!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.43", "verse_number": 43, "en": "SECOND INITIATE: Just what we set out to accomplish!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.44", "verse_number": 44, "en": "THIRD INITIATE: And better done than we could have done it!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.45", "verse_number": 45, "en": "S.: Remember, then, this lesson: That in all good works inclining to brotherhood, especially that which inclineth to spirit communion, mortals are ever assisted and guided by wise spirits of noble aspirations.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "Agoquim said: These are My divisions for the twelve feasts of the Gods of heaven. Observe ye them in My name, for they are My inheritance, bestowed to enrich the wisdom of men.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "All labor is Mine, saith Agoquim. By My hand moveth all that is.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "The earth is My footstool, and I have made it square to the north and south, east and west.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "Three things made I: air, earth and water. The angle with three sides made I in remembrance thereof. Three things bestowed I on man: the earth body, which is dark, and none can see through it; the spirit which liveth after the earth body is dead; the third part I gave to man is the soul, which joineth him to Me.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "The sun I made, and I set him high in the firmament that I might see from afar and behold My earth-born children. And the lakes and forests and rivers, and the caves in the earth, made I for man.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "All that I have made is good, and round, or square, according to the place I made for it.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "But in the very young days of the world, the impatience of men led them to perceive not Me and My good works, and they set about to do My labor. Thus came evil into the world; but I separated the evil from the good. Into the four corners of the world separated I them. I gave to the evil, where all is coldness and hunger; where they have invented the evil-killing bow and the flint-dart, destroying My living creatures.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "My righteous shall live in mounds of earth and stone and wood, where the Ugha (serpent and tiger and all other evil-devouring beasts) can not come upon them.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "But for the light of My angels to come and abide with My people, ye shall provide the hoogadoah, the well-covered house, and it shall have but one door, and pieces shall be put therein, so that when My chosen are within, all shall be dark, that My angels may teach them.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "But the dwelling of the Hoonshawassie (the prophet) shall have one Ongji (window).", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "And he shall have a Mukagawin, the true sign of poverty, and it shall be his jewel, made of copper and gold. For whom I have made to live in poverty in this world will I exalt in heaven. Only the poor have I made to be My prophets.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.12", "verse_number": 12, "en": "Over the mounds My angels shall keep watch against the evil approaching Ugha, and they shall awaken My sleeping children, and they shall not suffer.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.13", "verse_number": 13, "en": "Agoquim said: Boats have I provided for the spirits of good men to rise to heaven in, and My angels shall accompany them to a place of light and rest.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.14", "verse_number": 14, "en": "Agoquim said: To the priests have I given authority to make My signs and symbols, and to bestow them on My righteous children.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "Baugh-gan-ghad said: I go forth. All who follow Me, are of Me. By will only is anything accomplished. That which I desire, I do. Without Me nothing is done. My name is MIGHT.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "B-G-G. said: Fear is failure. I am without fear. I am the All Es, incorporated on the low earth, having no fear. (The All Es gave forth, having no fear. By this created He the creation. Like a lion goeth He forth. The sun is under His feet; so is the broad earth. Whatsoever liveth or is dead (inanimate objects), fleeth before His breath. Even Death cometh and goeth by His command.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "B-G-G. said: Go forth, My sons and daughters, multiply, fearing naught. Inasmuch as ye do these things, so will I exalt you. Inasmuch as ye do not these things, I will bind and enslave.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "B-G-G. said: Think not I choose a weak man or a coward to be king. I choose only the valiant, that fear not. Think not that I exalt the weak in heaven and make them Gods over mortals; I choose the slayer of men and the death-dealing.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "B-G-G.: Who dareth My power? Down on your bellies and crawl like serpents before My throne! Down and speak! I command!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "First Initiate: By Rux I swear! I will not down!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "Second I.: By Him who standeth above the Sun, I will not down.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "Third I.: Nor I, though thou drinkest my blood.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "B-G-G.: Then must I from My throne of skulls descend to raise the seat another row. Bring here my battle-ax (to servant). (The ax is brought, and the Master, clothed in a lion's skin, with a serpent's skin coiled about his head, descendeth from the throne, and seizing the battle-ax, falleth upon the initiates, who have been previously drilled. During the ceremonies, some of the initiates are killed outright, generally not intentionally. A cellar is underneath, and over this the boards are made to trip. The Master knoweth this, and (the initiates not knowing it) hath the advantage. The fray of skill lasteth about an hour, during which the initiates are mostly sent below.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "B-G-G.: Ha'oot! Ha'oot! With Ghads I fought. With Ghads I am king and brother! Come forth, fallen foes, and live triumphantly! With brothers like these I will go forth and gather skulls to build another temple. (The servants help all that are alive out of the cellar, and if they are well and strong they are spared, but if they are maimed bodily, they are slain with the battle-ax. All that thus fail to take the first degree, have their skulls scraped and added to the throne, or judgment-seat. During the ceremonies, and along with the initiates, are two or more who have been previously initiated, and these of course are spared.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "First I.: To Thee and none else, I swear everlastingly. Thine be forever the place of skulls. (Ug'sa'sa) (Golgotha.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.12", "verse_number": 12, "en": "B-G-G.: Know, then, ye Iod'a (Gods), I am descended from Baugh-ghan-ghad, the All Spirit of Light and Power. By Him incarnate in Mi, virgin of the corporeal world, My blood is fed by the souls of men. In the days before the flood of waters My Sire built a temple a hundred goo'en square; with skulls built he it, and feasted on their souls, uprising in the firmament, above the sun, companion of the stars. On the spirits of mortals slain, His spirit feasted full of rich strength, till all the world bowed down and called Him Master!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.13", "verse_number": 13, "en": "Oruhk (the choristers from behind, concealed): All hail! All hail! O Master, man and chief of blood! We come from the abode of flying wolves and lions and bears, upraised in the Osk'oe (sky) rocks that feed the black earth, the thrones of mighty Ghads! Great Baugh-ghan-ghad called us forth; for the clang of thy battle-ax shook the stars, saying: Down! Down, O ye Ghads, to Mi (mother earth). My holy begotten Son, king of men, doth feed His throne with dead men's skulls! Behold, O King of Men, these thy valiant fighters, from their birth, are reared as brother Ghads! O, slay them not, but teach them that he who would rule in heaven must begin on earth, and send his neighbors bellowing down to dust! Bring them before the altar, smoking in their blood, and on the ark (a chest which containeth incense) let them mingle in covenant with the Everlasting Son!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.14", "verse_number": 14, "en": "Kohen (The chief priest): On this tablet write ye your names in blood and by these bones and skulls swear ye.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.15", "verse_number": 15, "en": "Initiates: On the tablet write we our names in blood, and by these bones and skulls we swear.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.16", "verse_number": 16, "en": "Kohen and Initiates (together): To keep sacred the oath under the thigh; to preserve the holy words of the feast of flesh and blood, for coming generations, that the Light and Glory of Baugh-ghan-ghad, the Creator, may shine forever. Amen!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.17", "verse_number": 17, "en": "Master: Hioot'a! Hioot'a! Behold, my Sire appeareth in a cloud of smoke and fire. Bow down your heads, M'ghan'a (the women), and invoke His mighty power!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.18", "verse_number": 18, "en": "Kohen'a (the high priests): O thou Almighty Baugh-ghan-ghad, Creator of heaven and earth, appear! appear! Thou that slayest all and feasteth thereon, appear! appear!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.19", "verse_number": 19, "en": "B-G-G.: As My Sire reigneth in heaven, so reign I on earth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.20", "verse_number": 20, "en": "Initiates: Hail, Holy Father, Baugh-ghan-ghad! Hail, Holy Son, Baugh-ghan-ghad!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.21", "verse_number": 21, "en": "B-G-G.: I command the earth!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.22", "verse_number": 22, "en": "I's.: The Son commandeth the earth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.23", "verse_number": 23, "en": "B-G-G.: I command the mighty waters.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.24", "verse_number": 24, "en": "I's.: The Son commandeth the mighty waters.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.25", "verse_number": 25, "en": "B-G-G.: I command the air above the earth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.26", "verse_number": 26, "en": "I's.: The Son commandeth the air above the earth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.27", "verse_number": 27, "en": "B-G-G.: For these are Mine to keep forever.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.28", "verse_number": 28, "en": "I's.: For these are the Son's to keep forever.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.29", "verse_number": 29, "en": "B-G-G.: I am the Holy Esa-au-gau-hoi. (Church militant.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.30", "verse_number": 30, "en": "I's.: The Son is the Holy Esa-au-gau-hoi.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.31", "verse_number": 31, "en": "B-G-G.: My holy labor is to subdue nations and tribes of men.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.32", "verse_number": 32, "en": "I's.: The Son's labor is to subdue nations and tribes of men.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.33", "verse_number": 33, "en": "B-G-G.: I make treaties or break them when I will.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.34", "verse_number": 34, "en": "I's.: The Son maketh or breaketh treaties when he will.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.35", "verse_number": 35, "en": "B-G-G.: For all things on the earth are Mine.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.36", "verse_number": 36, "en": "I's.: For all things on the earth are His.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.37", "verse_number": 37, "en": "B-G-G.: I am immaculate, and can not do wrong.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.38", "verse_number": 38, "en": "I's.: The Son is immaculate, and can not do wrong.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.39", "verse_number": 39, "en": "B-G-G.: Right and Wrong must be judged by me.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.40", "verse_number": 40, "en": "I's.: Right and Wrong must be judged by the Son.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.41", "verse_number": 41, "en": "B-G-G.: By My will do I make Wrong right, or Right wrong.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.42", "verse_number": 42, "en": "I's.: By the Son's will maketh He Wrong right, or Right wrong.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.43", "verse_number": 43, "en": "B-G-G.: I am the All Holy Standard.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.44", "verse_number": 44, "en": "I's.: The Son is the All Holy Standard.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.45", "verse_number": 45, "en": "B-G-G.: I can not err. I and My Father are one.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.46", "verse_number": 46, "en": "I's.: The Son can not err. The Son and the Father are one.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.47", "verse_number": 47, "en": "B-G-G.: I now demand a thousand skulls.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.48", "verse_number": 48, "en": "I's.: The Son demandeth a thousand skulls.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.49", "verse_number": 49, "en": "B-G-G.: Baugh-ghan-ghad demandeth a thousand skulls.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.50", "verse_number": 50, "en": "I's.: Baugh-ghan-ghad shall have a thousand skulls.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.51", "verse_number": 51, "en": "B-G-G.: Baugh-ghan-ghad demandeth a pool of human blood for His Son's silver boat.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.52", "verse_number": 52, "en": "I's.: Baugh-ghan-ghad shall have a pool of human blood for His Son's silver boat.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.53", "verse_number": 53, "en": "B-G-G.: Go forth, my brother Iod'a (Gods), and bring the skulls and the blood, for this is the time of the sacred solemn feast!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.54", "verse_number": 54, "en": "I's.: We will forth and bring a thousand skulls and the blood for our King. All hail! Mighty King of Men! All Hail!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "These that follow being the first three rites of Emethachavah, that is to say:", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "M'git'ow said: A baker came to sell me bread. I said: Sell thy bread to my wife. She and I are one.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "The baker said: The two are one?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "This was the beginning of wisdom. I went into a field and heard the birds singing:", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "The Unknown said: The twain are one!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "I said: Couldst Thou, O Father, make all men as one, there would be peace and joy! No war!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "The Unknown said: Make thou the Family of the Ancients! Husband and wife shall be thy key. Husband, wife and children shall be thy model.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "I said: I can not make all men think alike, act alike, do alike.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "A vision came upon me. I saw a score of musicians, and every one had a different instrument, and they set about tuning their instruments, every one his own way, saying: Save we have our instruments tuned as one instrument, there can be no music; tune, therefore, thine instrument to mine.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "After a long while they wearied, for they were no nearer than at first. And every one said: It is impossible to attune them! It is useless trying more.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "The Unknown spake out of the firmament, saying: Choose ye one instrument first, and then attune another one to it. This accomplished, attune then a third instrument to them; after that, a fourth, and so on, and ye shall be all attuned alike.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.12", "verse_number": 12, "en": "Thereupon the musicians set to work, but could not agree whose should be the first instrument.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.13", "verse_number": 13, "en": "A pillar of fire descended from heaven and stood in the midst of the musicians; and in the center of the pillar of fire there appeared an instrument called the All Perfect.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.14", "verse_number": 14, "en": "The instrument gave forth one note, and all the musicians attuned to it. The Voice said: I have given the key-note, find ye the rest.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.15", "verse_number": 15, "en": "The pillar of fire departed. The instruments thus attuned in harmony played rapturously. The vision then departed.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.16", "verse_number": 16, "en": "This I perceive: to make the man and wife one; to make the man, wife and child one; to make the village one; to make the state one; to make the empire one; all in harmony, as one instrument, can not be done without a Central Sun, a Creator, to attune to. When a man is attuned to Him, and a woman attuned to Him, they will themselves be as one. When the family and the village are attuned to Him, it is easy. Without Him harmony can not be.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.17", "verse_number": 17, "en": "He, the Creator, then, must be first in all things, first in all places. He must be the nearest of all things, nearest of all places. In our rites and ceremonies He must be the All Ideal Perfection! the embodiment of a Perfect Person.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.18", "verse_number": 18, "en": "Emblem of worlds moldering into dust and of the harvest of souls ascending to the etherean realms above; emblem of mine own mortal dissolution and of the ascent of my soul, but whither, O Jehovih!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.19", "verse_number": 19, "en": "In Thee, Jehovih, will I have faith. In Thee only. Emeth (Faith) shall be my name, the foundation of my soul.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.20", "verse_number": 20, "en": "Henceforth I will eschew fame and the applause of men. Mine hands will I fold against earthly fame, and in Thy name, prepare my soul for heaven!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.21", "verse_number": 21, "en": "East: Over all the earth gave I dominion into man's hands; but I rule over man both on earth and in heaven, saith Jehovih. I fashioned the earth and breathed upon it, and man came forth a living soul. The veins in his body I made, and I made him conscious that he is. I speak into his ear, and hold up Mine hand before him that he may not err, but rejoice that he liveth and that he may glorify Me and My works. But he turneth aside and boasteth there is none greater than man.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.22", "verse_number": 22, "en": "P. and I.: O Jehovih, mighty in heaven, hallowed be Thy name! May I never forget Thee nor set myself up against Thee nor Thy kingdoms! (One bell.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.23", "verse_number": 23, "en": "E.: Who are these that call on Jehovih's name?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.24", "verse_number": 24, "en": "P.: Wanderers on the earth who are unappeased with corporeal things. From Corpor to Es we turn in Jehovih's name.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.25", "verse_number": 25, "en": "E.: For what purpose are ye in this holy chamber?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.26", "verse_number": 26, "en": "I.: To be initiated into the light and dominions of heaven.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.27", "verse_number": 27, "en": "E.: Have ye been prepared for this?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.28", "verse_number": 28, "en": "I.: Yea.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.29", "verse_number": 29, "en": "E.: How have ye been prepared?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.30", "verse_number": 30, "en": "I.: We abstain from flesh food and from strong drink, and endeavor to purify our corporeal bodies.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.31", "verse_number": 31, "en": "E.: Man may be pure in the corporeal body, but unprepared to receive the wisdom of high heaven. How else have ye been prepared?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.32", "verse_number": 32, "en": "I.: We have witnessed the spirits of the dead who have returned to the earth, reclothed in corporeal appearances. We have talked with them in the presence of witnesses.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.33", "verse_number": 33, "en": "E.: My friends, since ye live on the earth, and can commune with the spirits of heaven, why are ye still unappeased?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.34", "verse_number": 34, "en": "I.: The people of the earth lust after the things of the earth, and deceive us on all occasions. The spirits who manifest are of little wisdom; they know but little of heaven.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.35", "verse_number": 35, "en": "E.: Since your faith in mortals is nothing, and your faith in spirits from heaven little more, how hope ye to gain wisdom and truth by calling on the name of Jehovih?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.36", "verse_number": 36, "en": "I.: We know there are angels in heaven who have risen above the bondage of earth. We aim to purify ourselves, that such may come to us and give us light. By attuning ourselves to Jehovih, we have faith that He will provide us according to our talents, which we received from His hand.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.37", "verse_number": 37, "en": "E.: Have ye not called for the spirits of the well-known?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.38", "verse_number": 38, "en": "I.: Alas, we find that spirits can assume any name and form, and so deceive us. We have no faith in names; they are as the wind. Some spirits have kingdoms in the lower heavens, and they seek to win subjects for themselves in time to come.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.39", "verse_number": 39, "en": "E.: How hope ye to distinguish free spirits from the bound?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.40", "verse_number": 40, "en": "I.: Spirits who talk of their own earthly lives, and of earthly things, are familiar spirits who abide with mortals. There are spirits above them who can come and teach us of things in heaven. By serving Jehovih, we hope to adapt ourselves to their presence.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.41", "verse_number": 41, "en": "E.: Have not Osiris, and Baal, and Dagon, and Ashtaroth, appeared in many places?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.42", "verse_number": 42, "en": "I.: It is so said, but they have shown no wisdom, nor have they come but through deceivers and persons who lust after earthly things.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.43", "verse_number": 43, "en": "E.: Save ye become workers for Jehovih, by lifting up your fellow-men, how hope ye for good angels to come and lift you up?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.44", "verse_number": 44, "en": "I.: What shall we do? Give us light?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.45", "verse_number": 45, "en": "E.: Make ye a covenant with Jehovih that your pledges may be recorded in high heaven. Consider your words, for Jehovih is ever present and heareth all done in His name. Angels are also present, who have no interest in the affairs of men, save for the redemption of their souls. That which ye swear shall ye do from this time forth forever!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.46", "verse_number": 46, "en": "P. and I.: This, then, be my covenant: I will serve Thee, O Jehovih, and no other God, nor Lord, nor any other person born of woman, who professeth to save the souls of men; nor will I pray to them or adore them; for by their names have the nations of the earth been accursed with war and destruction. But to Thee only, O Jehovih, will I pay homage and adoration, now and forever. For Thee will I labor, by doing righteously toward all men, and equally with all these my brethren, the Emeth (Faithists) in one Everlasting Father, Creator of worlds! With all my might, and my wisdom and love, will I strive to enlighten my fellow-men and to lift them up out of darkness. More shall my labor be for others than for myself, henceforth forever! And my substance shall be given to relieve the helpless and distressed. Help me, O Jehovih, and make me strong in this my covenant, now and forever. Amen.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.47", "verse_number": 47, "en": "E.: This is the Dawn! Behold the rising Sun!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.48", "verse_number": 48, "en": "Ye are now on the road of everlasting light. Swerve not from your covenant with Jehovih, and the spirits from the second heaven will abide with you. He who taught you before will invest you with, etc.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.49", "verse_number": 49, "en": "In the name of Eolin, and by His power and wisdom vested in me, do I receive thee as my brother (or sister, as the case may be) of the Ancient Order of Dawn of the Brotherhood of Emeth, and I salute thee with the rod, etc.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "Dang: Let the Faithist remember his Father in heaven. The rising Sun adorneth the East.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "Dang: Give voice, O ye that remember Him. His name is a power in heaven and earth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "O. and I.: In Jehovih's name! (The salutation and signs withheld from publication.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "D.: Before proceeding on the road of everlasting life, let the Hyarh measure the bread and meal, that far-distant regions may not entrap us in want. If the measure be short, then we must replenish.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "Arise, O Children of Dawn! Come to the west of the altar, O Children of Dawn! (This do they.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "East: (with sound of gong, twice): Hear us, O Jehovih! Thy voice be with us. Thy wisdom guide us. Thy love exalt us. As we hope for Thy exalted angels to come and minister to us, O show us, Mighty and All Perfect, how we can best minister to others who are less fortunate than ourselves. Sting us, O Jehovih, as with wasps and nettles, if we fail to devote our whole time to the lifting up of our fellow-men. Thou hast measured all things, and made wisely. Hearken to me, O Jehovih.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "East: Jehovih saith: As a builder measureth the place for the house and provideth a sure foundation, let not My chosen neglect the All Light that I have given to every soul. Ye are as the stones in one building in My sight; and every one must be squared for the place I fashioned from the beginning. The Emeth'a shall be My house; to them will I give the whole earth. Yea, all others shall fail; but My chosen shall not fail.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "Jehovih saith: There have been idolaters from the time of the ancients to this day. Yea, they have had idols of stone and wood and brass; and they have had spirits in heaven and hell, before whom they bowed down and worshiped, but I put their idols away and none can find them. See to it, O My beloved, that ye be searched out and made upright before Me.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "Hyarh: Speak, O Emeth'a! Before the Father, raise up your voices. What was the building of the house of Dawn? How stood the temple? How stood the spirit chamber thereof?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "H.: By what right are ye in this chamber of light?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "Em.: By right of our covenant with Jehovih. Behold the ark! Jehovih's name we have written.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.12", "verse_number": 12, "en": "H.: What was done at Dawn in the East?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.13", "verse_number": 13, "en": "Em.: A philosopher passed twice about the table of the ancients. He held the key of many in one, and disclosed it before our eyes. After that he lighted the incense and departed. Then we covenanted, swearing an oath unto Jehovih, which was duly deposited, and thereupon were proclaimed under a Hawitcha with Dhargot, and then saluted Children of Dawn.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.14", "verse_number": 14, "en": "H.: Are ye still steadfast in your oath?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.15", "verse_number": 15, "en": "Em.: Yea.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.16", "verse_number": 16, "en": "H.: What more desire ye?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.17", "verse_number": 17, "en": "Em.: More light from heaven!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.18", "verse_number": 18, "en": "H.: Of the earth shall man learn earthly things. But light from heaven descendeth to mortals by the angels in heaven, who are servants of the Most High!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.19", "verse_number": 19, "en": "East: As the sun at noon is to the corporeal earth, so is Jehovih to the souls of men. As the eye turneth to the east to behold the rising sun, so turneth the spirit of the Child of Dawn to Jehovih, the Great Spirit.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.20", "verse_number": 20, "en": "North: He hath uttered the sacred name. His hands have made the signs of the ancients. His feet have run quickly to the afflicted, and his substance lifted them up.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.21", "verse_number": 21, "en": "East: As the wicked find strength in armies, so, not single-handed, laboreth the Child of Dawn.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.22", "verse_number": 22, "en": "North: He hath united in a brotherhood with many. In the chamber of holies supplicated he the Great Spirit. The words of enchantment are at his tongue's end; with his own hand writeth he on stone the pass-word of the Faithist. It is the circumference of all. It extendeth from left to right, and from below and upward, and it holdeth the symbol of life.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.23", "verse_number": 23, "en": "East: I am the Self within all selfs, saith Jehovih. Nor things seen, nor things unseen, nor light and darkness are, nor were, but all things proceed out of Me, and are of Me. Into motion and life quickened I them, and all of them are but members of My body.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.24", "verse_number": 24, "en": "East: I made man a spirit, but I gave him a corporeal body that he might learn corporeal things, saith Jehovih. Death I made that man might inherit My etherean worlds in the firmament of heaven.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.25", "verse_number": 25, "en": "East: But man turned away from Me and desired the things of earth; and after death his spirit was bound to that which he desired, and he became a wandering spirit on the face of the earth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.26", "verse_number": 26, "en": "Em.: In the midst of my desires I am cast down. I am unworthy in thy sight, O Jehovih! Have I not set store on my house and my land, and on all manner of earthly things? Yea, as with a chain, have I bound my spirit to become a wanderer on the earth?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.27", "verse_number": 27, "en": "East: Man buildeth a kingdom, and he gathereth riches, saith Jehovih. Yea, he warreth against his brethen for the glory of the king and chief ruler, and the profane man. For opinion's sake they slay one another outright.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.28", "verse_number": 28, "en": "East: Jehovih said: The spirit of the rich man have I bound, and the warrior, and great captain, and the man of renown, and the leader of men; and his spirit can not rise up from the earth. A companion have I made him in the lower heavens to the wanton and the drunkard, to the chaotic and foul-smelling.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.29", "verse_number": 29, "en": "Em.: Never, O Jehovih, will I raise up my hand against any man; nor will I war for the king, nor chief ruler, nor for any man, nor for any land, nor house, nor earthly thing. And though I be placed in front of battle, yet will I not take any man's life! So help me, O Father in heaven.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.30", "verse_number": 30, "en": "East: And the spirit of the bound have I made to encompass the earth in all places, and until they put away the darkness that is in them, they shall not inherit My etherean worlds, saith Jehovih. Neither shall they hear My voice, nor believe in My person, for they have descended into darkness and are blind and dumb to My glories in the firmament of heaven.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.31", "verse_number": 31, "en": "East: But I provided for them in their darkness, saith Jehovih. For I bring the earth into a light region in the firmament above, and I send My holy angels to deliver them out of darkness and bondage. In My name raise they up men with eyes to see and ears to hear, and I proclaim unto all the nations of the earth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.32", "verse_number": 32, "en": "Em.: Make of my body and my spirit, O Jehovih, a servant unto Thee; and whatsoever Thou puttest upon me, that will I accept and do with all my might and wisdom and love. That which Thou hast taught me, will I proclaim, fearing naught that may come upon me; for Thou shalt make of me an example of steadfastness for Thy glory. For the loss of my earthly Gods will I complain not; nor for imprisonment nor toil, nor sickness nor death! To Thy servant these things are as nothing. Without Thee, O Jehovih, I am as nothing; but Thy servant will I be henceforth forever. Amen!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.33", "verse_number": 33, "en": "Hyarh: My friends, what was the light of the ancients?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.34", "verse_number": 34, "en": "Em.: To learn the earth and call if Ah! To learn the sky and call it Oh! To learn the unseen power, as the wind, and call it E-O-Ih!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.35", "verse_number": 35, "en": "H.: Which was the secret pass-word to the lodge of light with the ancients? What was next?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.36", "verse_number": 36, "en": "Em.: To worship Eloih; to do no murder; to keep holy one day in seven; to steal not and to commit no adultery.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.37", "verse_number": 37, "en": "H.: What was the next light?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.38", "verse_number": 38, "en": "Em.: To do not unto others that which we desire not done unto us.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.39", "verse_number": 39, "en": "H.: What next?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.40", "verse_number": 40, "en": "Em.: To do unto others even as we desire them to do unto us; to love one's neighbor as one's self; to return good for evil.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.41", "verse_number": 41, "en": "H.: A greater light I now give unto you, in the name of Jehovih, which is: THAT YE SHALL DO GOOD UNTO OTHERS WITH ALL YOUR WISDOM AND STRENGTH, ALL THE DAYS OF YOUR LIVES; AND THAT YE SHALL PERCEIVE NO EVIL IN ANY MAN, NOR WOMAN, NOR CHILD, BUT IN THEIR BIRTH AND SURROUNDINGS.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.42", "verse_number": 42, "en": "Em.: This, also, do I accept of Thee, O Jehovih. Help me to swerve not, O Thou Light of my soul, for Thy glory and for the glory of Thy dominions, forever!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "SOUTH: If a man do evil, it taketh root in him, and will be entailed on his spirit, even into the next world. But if a man practice righteousness it will also take root, and his spirit will become as a star of glory in heaven.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "WEST: If a man court evil companions in this world, he shall find them also in the spirit world (Es). But if he constantly seek Jehovih, to and do his will, he shall find happiness both here and hereafter.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "NORTH: If a man minister unto others, so will he be ministered unto by the angels of heaven. But whosoever would grow in all gifts, let him labor to become one with the Father, and the Father will grow in him forever.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "EAST: Naked bring I man into the world, saith Jehovih. But I provide others for him in his infancy, that he may be fed and clothed. But when he is strong and wise, I command him to provide for himself, that he may be an honor and glory in My handiwork.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "Let no man shirk from the trials I put upon him, for in so doing he robbeth his own soul.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "W.: Who cometh here?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "I.: A Faithist in Jehovih.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "W.: Who is Jehovih?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "I.: The Great Spirit. He who is over all and within all. The Potent and Unseen. He it is whose Ever Presence quickeneth into life all that live.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "W.: Where is Jehovih?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "I.: Everywhere, nor is there place without Him.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.12", "verse_number": 12, "en": "W.: What is His form?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.13", "verse_number": 13, "en": "I.: No man can attain to know His form.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.14", "verse_number": 14, "en": "W.: What is His extent?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.15", "verse_number": 15, "en": "I.: No man can attain to know His extent.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.16", "verse_number": 16, "en": "W.: Is He Person?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.17", "verse_number": 17, "en": "I.: Inasmuch as all the living are persons, so is He the All Person of all things. Inasmuch as His Ever Presence quickened into life all that live, so is His Ever Presence with the living, seeing, hearing and feeling every word and act of all men, women and children on earth and in heaven.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.18", "verse_number": 18, "en": "W.: What interest hast thou in Him?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.19", "verse_number": 19, "en": "I.: He is my Father! He is within my soul. I am within Him, and a very member of His person.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.20", "verse_number": 20, "en": "W.: Whence came His name?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.21", "verse_number": 21, "en": "I.: As the wind whispereth E in the leaves, and uttereth O in the ocean's surge and in the thunder above, and Ih in the winter's shrill whistle, so came the name E-O-Ih, which hath become Jehovih, and Eloih, and Elohim and Wenohim.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.22", "verse_number": 22, "en": "W.: How was He discovered?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.23", "verse_number": 23, "en": "I.: The angels of heaven taught man of Him.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.24", "verse_number": 24, "en": "W.: Who are the angels of heaven?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.25", "verse_number": 25, "en": "I.: People who once lived on earth or other corporeal worlds?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.26", "verse_number": 26, "en": "W.: What is their form?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.27", "verse_number": 27, "en": "I.: Even as mortals, but being perfect.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.28", "verse_number": 28, "en": "W.: Where is heaven?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.29", "verse_number": 29, "en": "I.: Worlds unseen by mortals fill the etherean firmament above. These worlds are heaven; these the spirit worlds; these the etherean worlds; these are the abodes of the spirits of the dead.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.30", "verse_number": 30, "en": "W.: How came the angels from heaven down to the corporeal earth?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.31", "verse_number": 31, "en": "I.: In the Dan'ha they come direct; at other times they come through familiar spirits?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.32", "verse_number": 32, "en": "W.: Who are familiar spirits?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.33", "verse_number": 33, "en": "I.: Our fathers, mothers, brothers, sisters and other relatives and friends who have not been long dead, and such other spirits as have not learned of or risen to the heavens above earth's atmosphere. Many of these abide on the earth and with mortals, some for a few years, some for a hundred, and some for a thousand or more years.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.34", "verse_number": 34, "en": "W.: What is an idol?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.35", "verse_number": 35, "en": "I.: Anything that is worshiped, having form and figure.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.36", "verse_number": 36, "en": "W.: Who is God?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.37", "verse_number": 37, "en": "I.: A spirit with a heavenly throne believed by people in darkness to be the Creator of all things. Familiar spirits have oft called themselves God, and so taught mortals.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.38", "verse_number": 38, "en": "W.: Who are Saviors?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.39", "verse_number": 39, "en": "I.: Familiar spirits who have kingdoms in atmospherea, which, by the ancients, was called the lower heaven. Saviors are tyrants who make slaves of other spirits, who believe in them. Their slaves are sent back to mortals as guardian angels or familiars, in order to make captives of mortals after death, to augment the Savior's kingdoms in atmosphere.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.40", "verse_number": 40, "en": "W.: How can a man escape the toils of false Lords, Gods, Saviors and familiars?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.41", "verse_number": 41, "en": "I.: He shall covenant with Jehovih every day of his life, and serve Him by doing good works unto others with all his wisdom and strength. Nor shall he call in prayer on the name of a Savior or God, nor any other spirit, but on the Great Spirit only. If he do this, and eschew contention and war, and leadership, and earthly gain, and earthly aggrandizement, then will etherean angels come and guard him in the name of Jehovih.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.42", "verse_number": 42, "en": "W.: Who are ethereans?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.43", "verse_number": 43, "en": "I.: Spirits who have risen above the bondage of the earth and its atmosphere; spirits who can come only to such mortals as have attained beyond earthly desires.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.44", "verse_number": 44, "en": "W.: Hath it not been said: Swear not at all?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.45", "verse_number": 45, "en": "I.: Jehovih saith: Swear unto thy Father in heaven every day of thy life; and if thou fail to keep thine oath, thou shalt renew it on the following day, with repentance. But in all swearing it shall be for perfecting one's self and for the glory of the Father.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.46", "verse_number": 46, "en": "W.: Thou hast spoken wisely. Go, then, to the east, and stand upright before Jehovih, and there pray or utter an anthem of praise unto Him, that He may be glorified in thee, and thy guardian angels have honor in their pupil.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.47", "verse_number": 47, "en": "M and I.: Give ear unto me, O Jehovih! The vanities of the earth have no countenance in my sight; they are as one cast down in the day of his triumph.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.48", "verse_number": 48, "en": "Neither doth flesh food enter my mouth; nor strong drink, nor substance that cometh from an animal, or is part thereof; nor fish, nor worms, nor anything that breathes or hath breathed. (And if the I. is a prophet he addeth, nor roots, nor seeds that grow in the field.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.49", "verse_number": 49, "en": "In the air and in the fruits thereof is my substance; into the air riseth my spirit upward unto Thee, O my Father in heaven!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.50", "verse_number": 50, "en": "Quicker hast Thou made my spirit than my flesh; quicker Thou my soul than the dust of the earth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.51", "verse_number": 51, "en": "Who can comprehend Thy handiwork? Who else but Thee knoweth the delight of my soul unto Thee? Verily art Thou the comfort of the living, the solace of the dying and the joy of the dead.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.52", "verse_number": 52, "en": "I was not, and I knew not. Thy hand moved and brought me forth, and lo, here I am! Yea, Thy spirit moved upon Mi (mother earth), and I am one of the fruits thereof.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.53", "verse_number": 53, "en": "How shall I glorify Thee; and in Thy service how shall I render Thee recompense?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.54", "verse_number": 54, "en": "E.: My brother (or sister), thou hast covenanted with our Father at Dawn and at High Noon, saying: That that thou hast taught me will I proclaim, fearing naught that may come upon me. Go forth, then, and see to it that thou turnest not backward. Neither shalt thou raise up thy hand to do any man hurt.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.55", "verse_number": 55, "en": "E.: Since the time of the ancients to this day the worshipers of the One Great Spirit have been persecuted and abused by idolaters of Gods and Saviors.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.56", "verse_number": 56, "en": "E.: If, therefore, enemies accuse thee or assault thee, hear thou them patiently, but press onward in peace to the end, and the Father will be with thee.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.57", "verse_number": 57, "en": "I.: Such is my will unto Thee, O Jehovih! Thou shalt be the song of my life, Thou, the theme of my delight. Unto Thee will I give praise without ceasing, and my prayers shall be without number.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.58", "verse_number": 58, "en": "I.: I will go forth proclaiming Thee and Thy works, O Jehovih! In Thee shall Thy children rejoice with great joy. Yea, the inhabitants of the earth shall turn away from evil and learn to glorify Thee!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.59", "verse_number": 59, "en": "They have beholden the Rising Sun and the High Noon, and the stars in the firmament above, and they shall sing unto Thee for Thy glorious works.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.60", "verse_number": 60, "en": "Thy unseen heavens will I proclaim, and for the inheritance thereof shall people eschew war and earthly aggrandizement.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.61", "verse_number": 61, "en": "Yea, all people shall rejoice with me for the glory of the earth, and for Thy kingdoms above. Unto Thee shall they sing with rejoicing all the days of their lives, and Thy voice shall come upon them.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.62", "verse_number": 62, "en": "B: Halt! vain man! Blasphemer of the Zarathustrian law! I have heard thy insulting tongue. Thou raisest up thy voice against the wisdom of the ancients. Know, then, that the All Spirit spake to Zarathustra, the All Pure, who was the incarnation of All Light, born of the virgin Mi. This is the Zarathustrian law; the holy light of earth and heaven; the Brahman religion. Whoever since then saith: Lo, I hear the Voice, or lo, I see the Hand, is a blasphemer, an imposter! Have a care of thy speech!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.63", "verse_number": 63, "en": "I.: I would teach man to be happy and to rejoice in the Father over all!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.64", "verse_number": 64, "en": "B.: Insulting man, and of a truth a most wicked teacher. Man should torture his flesh, and by long fasts and abhorrent labor, make the earth hateful in his sight. Do thou not this, and thou shalt return to the earth a wandering spirit to afflict mortals.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.65", "verse_number": 65, "en": "I.: Nay, I would make the labor of earth a joy and thanksgiving, having faith in the Great Spirit, Jehovih!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.66", "verse_number": 66, "en": "B.: O Brahma! O Zarathustra! Go, barbarian! Out of my sight! Thou insulter of the ancients! Thou contaminatest the sacred land of Brahma! Begone, begone! Thou that reverest not the caste of men!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.67", "verse_number": 67, "en": "I.: Rejoice, O ye inhabitants of the earth. Jehovih liveth and reigneth, and ye are a glory within His works!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.68", "verse_number": 68, "en": "Fear not, O my beloved. His hand is over all. His wisdom knoweth your just deserts.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.69", "verse_number": 69, "en": "Go not backward, O all ye people. He is ever at your hand in wisdom and strength.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.70", "verse_number": 70, "en": "A nurse provided He for the newborn, and the spirit of the full-grown man is in his keeping.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.71", "verse_number": 71, "en": "With your eyes, O my beloved, go not back to the ancients, nor seal not up your souls, and He will give you light.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.72", "verse_number": 72, "en": "Arouse ye up, O ye inhabitants of the earth! He is the same, yesterday, today and forever!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.73", "verse_number": 73, "en": "BU: Hold! Thou man of darkness! Barbarian, hold thy tongue! Insulter of the ancients! Blasphemer against great Buddha! Who can hear the Voice? Thou!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.74", "verse_number": 74, "en": "The All Light descended to the earth incarnate form in Gotama Buddha. Who'er since then saith: I can hear the Voice, is an imposter, a breeder of revolt against all truth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.75", "verse_number": 75, "en": "I.: I proclaim the living Presence.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.76", "verse_number": 76, "en": "Bu.: Heedest thou not the Ahura'Mazdian law! To establish Buddha on earth, more than a hundred millions have been slain in war! This day the Buddhists have twenty million soldiers. Wouldst thou insult this mighty power? Turn the nations loose in blasphemous song?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.77", "verse_number": 77, "en": "I.: I would lift man up from the bondage of the ancients.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.78", "verse_number": 78, "en": "Bu.: Nay, begone, or thy blood be upon thy head. Thou defilest the holy land of Buddha. Out of my dominions! thou that heedest not the caste of men! Begone!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.79", "verse_number": 79, "en": "I.: But hear me---(The I. succeedeth in passing him.) Bu.: Nay, I will not. I will scourge thee with stripes! (Whipping him.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.80", "verse_number": 80, "en": "(As he graspeth the chastening rod the I. maketh his escape. Bu. retireth to his druk. The I. goeth forth again.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.81", "verse_number": 81, "en": "I.: Better art Thou, O Jehovih, our Living Father, than the dead ancients. Better Thy love than the dominion of the whole earth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.82", "verse_number": 82, "en": "Who shall fear when our Father in heaven is also within us. Who shall take anger to his soul for the darkness of men?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.83", "verse_number": 83, "en": "Glorify Him, O all ye people, in praise and thanksgiving. He speaketh in the song of the bird. He painteth the flowers of the field with His own hand.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.84", "verse_number": 84, "en": "Of all the living, man alone turneth away from Thee! Man alone setteth up an idol on earth or imagineth one in heaven, and worshipeth it.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.85", "verse_number": 85, "en": "Proclaim Him with great joy, O my beloved; there is but one All Person, the Great Spirit, Jehovih.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.86", "verse_number": 86, "en": "C.: Fanatic! Infidel! Blasphemer against Christ! Sayest thou God hath lungs and lips, and a voice, and thou canst hear Him? O thou insulter of this enlightened age!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.87", "verse_number": 87, "en": "Only the ancient prophets could hear Him. Then God descended to the earth, incarnated himself in a woman, and was born of a virgin, becoming Christ, the Savior of the world! Christ is the lamb of peace! He is the fountain of love. Christ saith: I am the life and the light!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.88", "verse_number": 88, "en": "I.: I know only one Great Spirit, Jehovih.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.89", "verse_number": 89, "en": "C.: Thou liest! Thou disturber of the peace! Thou infidel to the holy book and Christian law! To establish Christ on earth, a hundred millions have been slain in war! Know thou, this day, the Christians have seven millions of soldiers, and the mightiest ships of war on the globe!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.90", "verse_number": 90, "en": "I.: I teach peace and love!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.91", "verse_number": 91, "en": "C.: Hold thy tongue, infidel! Christ saith: Think not I am come to send peace on earth; I am not come to send peace, but a sword; Christ saith: I come to set man at variance against his father, and a daughter against her mother, and a daughter-in-law against her mother-in-law.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.92", "verse_number": 92, "en": "Before Christ shall every knee bow and every tongue confess him Lord of all. Down on thy knees, infidel, and confess to him. Down!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.93", "verse_number": 93, "en": "I.: Never. To none but great Jehovih.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.94", "verse_number": 94, "en": "C.: O blasphemer against the Lord! Now will I bind thee for heresy behind prison bars, and thy sacred books confiscate.
