(He seizeth a cord.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.95", "verse_number": 95, "en": "I.: Nay, bind me not. I would but raise up my fellow-men.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.96", "verse_number": 96, "en": "C.: Dog! Desecrator of religious liberty, begone out of this fair land of meekness and love!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.97", "verse_number": 97, "en": "I.: Hear ye Him, O all ye people of the earth, and ye of heaven above. His voice is in the air, and in the stones of the earth. Yea. He speaketh in the sun and moon and stars! All things in heaven and earth are the expression of His soul.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.98", "verse_number": 98, "en": "Make merry, O ye inhabitants of the earth. Jehovih is Ever Present, and none can counterfeit Him. Open your ears that ye may hear His Voice, and your eyes that ye may behold His Person!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.99", "verse_number": 99, "en": "M: Foolish man! Thou talkest to the wind. Only the ancient prophets could hear the voice. Mohammed was His prophet. Go, then, read the prophets of old, and study the Koran. Since Mohammed, no more wisdom can come into the world.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.100", "verse_number": 100, "en": "I.: I would raise up the ignorant and debased.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.101", "verse_number": 101, "en": "M.: Nay, thou art a fool! The ignorant and debased are as God made them. They are content; disturb them not.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.102", "verse_number": 102, "en": "I.: I would make their labor light.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.103", "verse_number": 103, "en": "M.: Be wise. Yesterday thou wert born; today thou livest; to-morrow thou diest, and the next day thou wilt be forgotten. Go, then, procure wine and women, and feast thyself while thou mayst.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.104", "verse_number": 104, "en": "I.: I teach a higher life.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.105", "verse_number": 105, "en": "M.: I want no higher life. This world is good enough for me. God is just. He hath provided heavens for angels. The earth he made for man, and to man gave he passions to be indulged. Otherwise he had not given them. Therefore I will have of the earth its sweetness whilst I may. God hath already made all things; all is finished. Man hath nothing to do but seek pleasure and die.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.106", "verse_number": 106, "en": "I.: I would make the life of the peasant a pleasant one.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.107", "verse_number": 107, "en": "M.: Thou disturbest my kingdom. To establish Mohammed, a hundred millions have been slain in war. The Mohammed hath this day two millions of soldiers. That is sufficient. Depart thou, then, out of the honest land of the Mohammed, and may God's holy book and the koran enlighten thee.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.108", "verse_number": 108, "en": "I.: Alas, O my Father, who art Ever Present! Yesterday I was puffed up with great joy, but the four quarters of the world (druks) have risen up against me. Today I am cast down in sorrow.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": [], "images": [{"src": "/plates/edition1882/image180.jpg", "edition": "1882"}]}, {"id": "cosmology.11.109", "verse_number": 109, "en": "The inhabitants of the earth are bound down by the little light of the ancients; but Thy Great Light of Living Presence they put afar off. What shall I do, O Father,that I may contribute to the founding of Thy kingdom on earth? O give me light, that my labor be not vain!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.110", "verse_number": 110, "en": "E. (with four bells): My friend, the Great Spirit beholdeth thee at all times and places; and since thou hast been faithful to Him, so have His etherean spirits ministered unto thee. And now that thou asketh for light from our Father, thy words are not in vain.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.111", "verse_number": 111, "en": "E.: Repair thou, then, into Dehabalizzah (Golden Chamber), where the voice of self and dispute never enter; and thou shalt learn of the mysteries of the dominions of the Gods and Saviors; after which thou shalt learn to apply thy labor that it not be vain, but profitable to the world and a glory to Jehovih in founding His kingdom.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.112", "verse_number": 112, "en": "I.: Angels of heaven lead the way!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.113", "verse_number": 113, "en": "E.: Who cometh here?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.114", "verse_number": 114, "en": "I.: One anxious to learn how best to serve the Father, that his life may be fruitful unto others.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.115", "verse_number": 115, "en": "VOICE FROM THE SPIRIT, OR A MORTAL REPRESENTATIVE STANDING ON THE RIGHT OF THE E.: How easier it is to bend a young sprout than a full grown oak! saith Jehovih. Have not the Gods and Saviors in the lower heavens discovered this, and so set guardian spirits and familiars over the newborn? What wonder is it that men are stiff-necked in the doctrines of the ancients? Spirits of darkness urge them on, and they delight in war and earthly dominions, for the benefit of their Gods and Saviors. Neither know they that they are servants to spirits of darkness; and they glorify themselves in caste, and in riches, and dominion, and in becoming rulers and chief leaders.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.116", "verse_number": 116, "en": "Jehovih saith: Let My chosen be wise after the manner of the self-Gods and Saviors, and also seek out infants and little ones and become guardians over them, but unto righteousness and good works, teaching them peace and love, and to live in harmony, and to abjure war and earthly profit.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.117", "verse_number": 117, "en": "As much as ye do this, especially teaching spirit communion, so do ye lay the foundation for My etherean spirits to come and abide with them as guardians during life, and even after death.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.118", "verse_number": 118, "en": "Neither shall ye circumscribe them in any of the talents I have given them, especially in the love of liberty, but perfect them in all things, teaching them to live in families of tens, or twenties, or hundreds, or thousands, even as in the olden times, holding all things in common, and being as brethren, one with another.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.119", "verse_number": 119, "en": "Waste not your time in discoursing with those who, having heard, will not practice My commandments. Nevertheless, to as many as come in your way, ye shall say: If ye can not curb your self-desires in this world, neither can ye in the next; and if ye can not live in a brotherhood of peace and love on earth, neither shall ye find a brotherhood of peace and love in heaven.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.120", "verse_number": 120, "en": "I.: I have heard Thy wisdom, O Jehovih, and I swear unto Thee, with all my soul, I will fulfill these Thy just commandments with all my wisdom and strength. Help me, O Father, to this end, for Thy Glory forever and ever. Amen!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.121", "verse_number": 121, "en": "E.: By Thy Power, Wisdom and Love, and in Thy Name, O Jehovih, do I receive this Thy son (or daughter), and proclaim him brother (or sister) of the Golden Chamber! In Thy name, O Father, I proclaim him in all the earth and in heaven above by these Thy solemn rites, for Thy glory. Amen!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": [], "images": [{"src": "/plates/edition1882/image181.jpg", "edition": "1882"}, {"src": "/plates/edition1882/image183.jpg", "edition": "1882"}]}, {"id": "cosmology.11.122", "verse_number": 122, "en": "The Panic name of the twenty-five signs.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.123", "verse_number": 123, "en": "To trace the names down through the languages to the present period.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.124", "verse_number": 124, "en": "To designate the place in the heavens where the present sign would be situated.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.125", "verse_number": 125, "en": "The position of the altar in the temple.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.126", "verse_number": 126, "en": "To give the cyclic dates of the signs.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.127", "verse_number": 127, "en": "What group of stars, according to the Panic names, gave the field 1, 2, 3, and of 4, 5, 6, and of all the others, and the period of time of grouping them?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.128", "verse_number": 128, "en": "Why is this degree called M'hak, or Golden Chamber?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.129", "verse_number": 129, "en": "Why were the four days in each moon set apart as mass days, or sacred days? At what period, according to the Panic names, was the first mass ordained on earth?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.130", "verse_number": 130, "en": "What was the position of the great serpent (solar phalanx) at that time?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.131", "verse_number": 131, "en": "To the last question the I. respondeth: Alas, O Avaya (priest), I have not traveled so far. The Avaya saith: Then I will prepare thee for another degree. The I. respondeth: So be it. Here endeth the M'hak degree, with music and prayer.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": [], "images": [{"src": "/plates/edition1882/image222.jpg", "edition": "1882"}]}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "S.: By what authority approachest thou the Chamber of Adepts?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "I.: As Jehovih created me, so I am.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "S.: What canst thou do?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "I.: I labor more than sufficient to provide for myself. I govern my passions and corporeal desires; I never speak in anger, or even think in anger, or hatred or envy. I never talk of myself, or what I can do, or what I have done, so as to laud myself. Neither do I seek to justify myself, by words or arguments, for errors which I may have committed. I contend with no one for anything, or principle, or knowledge of my own. I administer unto others for their corporeal and spiritual resurrection and happiness.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "S.: Wouldst thou not contend for right and justice, against wrong and bondage?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "I.: Alas, I might be in error as to what is right or wrong, or justice, or bondage. Nay, I contend not at all; neither do I resist; nor set up my judgment against any man's. The light of the Father which I receive, that declare I unto others.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "S.: What else hast thou attained?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "I.: To adapt myself to others, so as to be desired by them. I have taught myself to love to wait on and assist the low and depraved; to wash them and provide them with comfort. I have overcome all desire to serve myself for self-sake in any way whatsoever.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "S.: What are thy possessions?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "I.: Nothing. I have put away all earthly possessions.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "S.: What are thy qualifications?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.12", "verse_number": 12, "en": "I.: Try me.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.13", "verse_number": 13, "en": "The initiate is now tried as to his capacity to see spiritually, whilst blindfolded, and to hear spiritually something beyond the reach of mortal ears. Then he exhibiteth the power of angels to write on the tablets before the altar, in presence of witnesses. After this the initiate asketh the angels to lift him to the ceiling, and this is also accomplished in presence of witnesses. Next he casteth himself in trance, and the angels come in sar'gis and talk to the people. After this he goeth out of his mortal body, traveling in spirit to a neighbor's house, seeing and hearing what is there, and making his spirit manifest unto that place; and then he returneth and re-inhabiteth his own body. Thus endeth the trial.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.14", "verse_number": 14, "en": "S.: Thou art found worthy of the most sacred Order of Adepts. What hopest thou to gain by this initiation?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.15", "verse_number": 15, "en": "I.: By meeting with like fellows, the soul of man findeth a haven of rest, after which man cometh forth again invigorated for arduous labor.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.16", "verse_number": 16, "en": "(After this, followeth the ceremony of passing the arch, and attaining to the foot of the throne of light, where the initiate maketh his vows to Jehovih. Music bells, etc., etc., omitted.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "God said: Before learning to approach the foot of the throne, what profit is it to show them the way to mount upon the throne itself?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "Preserve, therefore, the secrets of prophecy for such as apply themselves to keeping Jehovih's commandments. Those who have attained to the foot, can of themselves find the way to mount upon it.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "In the time of Moses, the people of Arabin'ya worshiped many Gods and Lords, whose high heavenly Captain was Osiris. Four chief Gods were under him; they were: Baal, Ashtaroth, Dagon and Ashdod. There were seven and twenty other Gods also, known to mortals.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "When the Israelites traveled forth amidst the different tribes, they were beset to know what Lord or what God they worshiped, and by what Lord or what God they were led forth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "The Light in wisdom and words came to Moses to say to the nations, ALAS, HIS NAME, WHOM WE WORSHIP, MAN DARE NOT UTTER.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "Within the commune families, were certain signs and pass-words belonging to the different degrees. There were also oral rules of life and worship, but these were kept secret from the multitude, but the instruction of the commune fathers to the families was by this method made to harmonize all the people.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "For general behavior, Moses gave ten commands, which were not only made public but were incumbent on the commune fathers to teach orally to their respective families. The following are the commandments thus taught, that is to say:", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "I AM THE I AM that brought thee out of Egypt.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "Thou shalt have no Gods nor Lords but the I AM.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "Thou shalt not make any image of the I AM out of anything that is in heaven above, or on the earth beneath, or in the waters.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "Thou shalt not bow down thyself before idols nor images, nor anything having the form of anything in heaven, or on the earth, or in the waters.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "Thou shalt not speak My name in public, for I will not hold him guiltless that giveth it to idolaters and lovers of evil.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "Remember the sacred days and keep them holy. Six days shalt thou labor; but the seventh day is the Sa'abbadha.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.12", "verse_number": 12, "en": "Honor thy father and mother.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.13", "verse_number": 13, "en": "Thou shalt not kill (any living thing).", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.14", "verse_number": 14, "en": "Thou shalt not commit adultery.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.15", "verse_number": 15, "en": "Thou shalt not steal; nor bear false witness; nor covet anything that is another's.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.16", "verse_number": 16, "en": "In those days it so happened that one Koetha, an Egyptian woman, a su'is, went into the lodge at noon, no person being within the lodge save herself. Now, whilst she was examining the remnants of shew-bread and basins, and the candlestick, a Voice spake to her, saying: TOUCH NOT THESE, THEY ARE SACRED. But the woman, knowing it was a spirit, said: If I tell what I have heard, I will be accursed; for was it not the multitude of seeresses that brought the plagues on Egypt?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.17", "verse_number": 17, "en": "Again the Voice spake, saying: I will give thee the signs and pass-words of the High Fathers, that they may also know that thou knowest. Now, thereupon the Spirit told her the secrets; and he likewise said: Say thou to Moses, THE I AM SAITH (and Moses will wonder at thy speech): Behold, these implements are sacred. Hide thou them by day, for they who have spirit perception, perceiving them, will obtain the signs and pass-words.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.18", "verse_number": 18, "en": "The woman, Koetha, did as commanded, and Moses commanded workmen to make a tabernacle (a model or image of a place of worship, a portable temple), and the sacred implements were kept within said tabernacle, and this was the corporeal base of the ark of Bon, a locality in the etherean heaven, by which the light of the sacred heaven reached the earth to Moses and his people, in a pillar of cloud by day and of light by night.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.19", "verse_number": 19, "en": "The secret ceremonies commanded vegetable diet for fathers, prophets, seers, and wanonas (trance subjects), and many of Koetha's people followed their example. After some years of experience, those who fasted from flesh murmured, saying: What have we gained for our sacrifice?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.20", "verse_number": 20, "en": "So they broke their fasts by hundreds and by thousands; and there came by their camps numberless birds, and they caught and killed and ate them freely; but, being unaccustomed to such diet, they were taken with fever and died, upward of sixty thousand of them, men, women and children, and the place was called Kibrath-Hattaavah, the place of lusters.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.21", "verse_number": 21, "en": "In the year 3269 B.K., the Israelites began to marry with the Canaanites, who were under the control of Baal.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.22", "verse_number": 22, "en": "Ashtaroth, whose dominions extended jointly with Baal over Western and Southern Arabin'ya, sent spirit emissaries to the handsomest of the women of Canaan, and by impression led them into the camps of the Israelites, to tempt the young men, for by these means did the heathen Gods determine to destroy the worshipers of the GREAT UNSEEN. And many of the Israelitish young men were tempted by the beauty of the heathen women and thus took them for wives; and said wives brought with them their own familiar spirits, who were slaves to Baal and Ashtaroth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.23", "verse_number": 23, "en": "The ark of Bon immediately sent Jerub, an etherean angel, into the Israelites' camp, giving him two thousand inspiring spirits to counteract the labors of Baal and Ashtaroth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.24", "verse_number": 24, "en": "Nevertheless, it so came to pass that when the half-breed children were grown up, having Canaan mothers, they began to murmur against the peace policy of the Israelites, saying: As other people have kings and emperors, why not we? As other people raise up soldiers, declare war, and go forth possessing themselves of lands and cattle, why do not we?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.25", "verse_number": 25, "en": "For three hundred and ninety-seven years after going out of Egypt, the Israelites lived without a corporeal king, or other government, save the community of fathers; and they attained to the number of six millions of souls, men, women and children.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.26", "verse_number": 26, "en": "But in the three hundred and ninety-seventh year, the Gods Baal and Ashtaroth triumphed, through their familiar spirits, and caused the Israelites to anoint a king to rule over them. This king was called Saul, signifying OF THE LORDGOD. Prior to this the Israelites acknowledged no God nor Lord, but covenanted with the Great Spirit, E-O-Ih. The name had been kept secret with the fathers, and the commandments were announced from the Great Spirit, I AM.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.27", "verse_number": 27, "en": "The familiar spirits now inspired Saul to change the words of the commandments to the LORD THY GOD, as a conciliatory stratagem to please the nations and tribes of people who worshiped Baal, Dagon, Ashtaroth, Haughak, and other Gods and Lords of the lower heavens.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.28", "verse_number": 28, "en": "Thus was substituted Lord God (Land God) for Jehovih (called sacredly, E-O-Ih), and as the Great Spirit had been heretofore taught to the Israelites as an inconceivable entity, WHOSE FORM AND EXTENT no man could attain to know, so was He now transformed and declared to be in the form of a man, and having a residence in the firmament of heaven. Thus they made the Great Spirit merely an idol; thus they began the overthrow of the holy doctrines of Moses.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.29", "verse_number": 29, "en": "Jerub, the etherean angel in command, sought to preserve the old tenets, and amongst mortals sought out one, Samuel, and inspired him to establish a college of prophets, and this was done at Naioth, and hither congregated the inspired; and they established a crescent Tablet, and made prophecies from etherea for a period of seventy years.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.30", "verse_number": 30, "en": "Hereupon the Israelites were twain, those of the king and those of E-O-Ih, the prophets, and these placed them at a disadvantage before neighboring tribes and nations. Having sacrificed the name of the Great Spirit, and made him into a man figure, the heathen said: What better is their Lord God than our Lord or our God? and they made war against the Israelites on all sides; and the latter having stooped to a corporeal king, went further on and raised armies of soldiers and went to war.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.31", "verse_number": 31, "en": "In the four hundred and ninth year after the departure out of Egypt, the entire PEACE POLICY and NON-RESISTANCE of the Mosaical Inspiration was overthrown, and the Israelites became warriors, and even warred against one another.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.32", "verse_number": 32, "en": "The Israelites not only worshiped the imaginary idol, Lord God in heaven, but built representatives of Him in stone and wood, and worshiped them also.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.33", "verse_number": 33, "en": "And now, one Ahijah, a young man from the college of prophets, foretold the coming captivity of the Israelites as a consequence of their idolatry.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.34", "verse_number": 34, "en": "In the year 2768 B.K., a young man, Elijah, was raised up from the branch of the college of prophets, and, under the inspiration of the angel Jerub, went forth preaching amongst the heathen, preaching not the Lord God, but Jehovih.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.35", "verse_number": 35, "en": "Ahab, a king, a worshiper of Baal, said to Elijah: What better is one God than another, or more powerful? Are not all Gods but the spirits of men raised up in heaven?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.36", "verse_number": 36, "en": "Elijah answered him, saying: I preach neither Lord nor God, but Jehovih, the Creator, who is Ever Present and Potent over all things.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.37", "verse_number": 37, "en": "Ahab said: Then thy Master hath neither eyes nor ears, knowing nothing, like the wind. He is foolish, and without intelligent answer to thy prayers.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.38", "verse_number": 38, "en": "Elijah said: Summon thou thy priests, and thy high priests, who have power through Baal and Ashtaroth and Dagon, and they and their Gods shall try in a tournament against Jehovih.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.39", "verse_number": 39, "en": "Ahab caused the tournament to take place, and when the priests and high priests of Baal had spread the sacrifice they repeated their invocations for a miracle to prove the power of Baal and the other Gods; but lo and behold, Jerub, the etherean angel, with ten thousand spirits to assist him, prevented any sign or miracle being accomplished by the familiar spirits of Baal and Ashtaroth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.40", "verse_number": 40, "en": "And now Elijah stretched forth his hand unto Jehovih, saying: If it be Thy will, O Father, give these people a sign of Thy Power!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.41", "verse_number": 41, "en": "And Jerub and his etherean host of angels, caused a flame of fire to descend on the altar and consume the sacrifice. The people feared, and many fell down, exclaiming, Jehovih is mighty! Jehovih is mighty!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.42", "verse_number": 42, "en": "When the tournament was ended, Elijah went upon Mount Carmel, and prayed for rain, and Jerub, with ten thousand times ten thousand angels, brought the winds from the north and south and east and west, and the moisture in the air above was converted into rain, and thus the long drought was ended.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.43", "verse_number": 43, "en": "In the year 2635 B.K., the college of prophecy raised up Jonah, and Amos, and Hosea.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.44", "verse_number": 44, "en": "In the year 2574 B.K., the kingdoms of Israel perished, and they became a scattered people, and fell into bondage again.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.45", "verse_number": 45, "en": "2439 years before kosmon, Manasseth, son of Hezekiah, established idol worship, and, by law, abolished the worship of Jehovih. He caused the prophet Isaah to be sawn in twain; and the judgment was, because he worshiped Jehovih.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.46", "verse_number": 46, "en": "For three hundred years following, the only Faithists amongst the Israelites were the prophets, and the followers of the prophets, who had never affiliated with those of the kingdoms and armies.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.47", "verse_number": 47, "en": "The Faithists proper were a small minority, and scattered in many lands. The rest, who were called Jews, lived under written laws and ceremonies, which were compiled and established by Ezra, in Jerusalem, which combination of books was called the Bible, and was completed in the year 2344 B.K.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.48", "verse_number": 48, "en": "From that time forward, the Jews became worshipers of THE LORD and THE GOD, but the scattered tribes of the Faithists still held to the Great Spirit, Jehovih, keeping their service secret. These latter were without sin, doing no war nor resistance of evil against evil, but returning good for evil, and loving one another as one's self.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.49", "verse_number": 49, "en": "From the Faithist branch sprang the Asenean (Essenean, or Es'eans) Association, cultivating prophecy and purity of spirit. For further light, read of Pharaoh in the lower heavens, and the migration of the East Indian Gods westward, in another part of Oahspe.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.50", "verse_number": 50, "en": "The angels of Jehovih now dwelt with the Aseneans, who were the true Israelites in fact. Though many of the Jews also professed Jehovih under the name of God and Lord God.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.51", "verse_number": 51, "en": "In three hundred and fifty years after this Jehovih raised up from the Aseneans, one Joshu, an iesu, in Nazareth. Joshu re-established Jehovih, and restored many of the lost rites and ceremonies.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.52", "verse_number": 52, "en": "In the thirty-sixth year of Joshu's age he was stoned to death in Jerusalem by the Jews that worshiped the heathen Gods.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.53", "verse_number": 53, "en": "Forty years after Joshu's death, a false God, Looeamong, with millions of angel emissaries, obsessed the inhabitants of all those countries and plunged them into war and anarchy.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.54", "verse_number": 54, "en": "The Faithists were scattered to the four ends of the earth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.55", "verse_number": 55, "en": "Jehovih said: Never more shall My chosen have kings. I alone am King.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.56", "verse_number": 56, "en": "And so it is to this day.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.57", "verse_number": 57, "en": "Looeamong, the false God, now changed his name and falsely called himself Christ, which is the Ahamic word for knowledge. And he raised up tribes of mortal warriors, who called themselves Christians, who are warriors to this day.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.58", "verse_number": 58, "en": "The doctrine of these warriors was, that knowledge, which implieth general education, was the best preventative against crime and misery. Neither understood any man in those days that the word Christ had any reference to a man or person. Now, from the time of Moses to Ezra, there was an interval of four hundred years, in which the Jews had no written record.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.59", "verse_number": 59, "en": "The age of the Ezra Bible is not, therefore, from the time of Moses, but from the time of Ezra, 2344 years B.K.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "Descended by the Yi-ha light through mortals, and in the Vedan Gods revealed from Zarathustra in Juian, Zend and Haizariyi, and thence into Vede, and thence into Sanscrit, and by Brahma and by Buddha. These, then, that follow, are the pillars of light of Great Jehovih, still standing as the remnants of His beloved sons and daughters in the East.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "Airyana, the good, created things. Anra'mainyus, the evil, created things. Gau, place of science in heaven. Maori, the second holy heaven. Bakhdhi, third holy place in heaven. Haroyu, confederate republics in heaven. Haetumat, emancipated heaven above the lower or bound heavens.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "Variena, a circle divided by cross-lines into four quarters; made for Thraetaono, a holy name, which had power over the Dahaka, serpent, i.e., evil.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "Nisai, faith, belief; a created place in the unseen heavens, which nurtureth man's soul, created by Ahura'Mazda, the good Creator.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "Kanthiuzhada, unbelief; a place in the unseen heavens which nurtureth unbelief in mortals, created by Anra'mainyus, the evil creator.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "Yatu, to sin against Ahura'Mazda, or against one's own being.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "Hapta Hendu, India; land of seven rivers.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "Daevas, all evil, and evil men in general. But a real and wholly acting Daeva is a sodomite.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "Druks Nagus, literally, rotting; also that decomposition of flesh which becometh food for insects and vermin.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "Crossha or Craosha, an angel sent by the Creator, speaking for Him.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "Druks and Daeva druks, sinful men.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "Tistrya, an angel from the still heavens who ruled the flocks of mortals. He was said to reside on Sirius; and that star was afterward named after him; and mortals afterward worshiped the star, forgetting the legend of their forefathers.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.12", "verse_number": 12, "en": "Ctara, or Gaura, who, in like manner, dwelt on a star, and was forgotten in the lapse of ages, and the star worshiped in his stead.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.13", "verse_number": 13, "en": "Zami, another angel who became a star in like manner.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.14", "verse_number": 14, "en": "Urvar, also a star thus named.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.15", "verse_number": 15, "en": "Gogpend, ditto. These different Gods were originally assigned certain labor on earth. One had charge of all growing things in the water; another of the purity of vegetable seeds; another of grain seeds; another of breeding of horses and cows; another of mortal marriages; another of young children, newborn. Yima, who was chief God over all the other Gods, thus assigned them places; and each and all these Gods of second degree had hosts of ministering spirits under them, and these were distributed and appointed over mortals as guardian angels, and, by inspiration and by other impressions, caused mortals to thus worship their masters, the Gods who claimed to reside on the stars. Thus did Yima teach mortals that through him, and his kingdoms only, could man prosper on earth, and rise in heaven after death. In course of time, however, the inhabitants of earth forgot the angels, and worshiped the stars instead.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.16", "verse_number": 16, "en": "Jahi, taurus, the bull. The God of force. In the Ebraic language this same God is called Jah. In course of time mortals forgot the origin of this God, and ignorantly supposed the name to be an abbreviation of Jehovih. In the Cuneiform inscriptions his name is called Bagho. The Panic origin is Taughad. (See Tablet Biene Poit, the figure of a bull, with man's face.) The spiritual meaning, force, or force of character, or energy to do, or decree with authority. As the pope issueth a Bull, of which the foregoing was the original.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.17", "verse_number": 17, "en": "Caoka, or gha-oka. Good, genial and amorous. (See Tablet Biene Poit, the figure of a ram.) He is also called Hoebah and goa-bah.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.18", "verse_number": 18, "en": "Airyana, a protector. (See tablet of Semoin.) In Tablet Biene he is made in the form of a lion, with man's face.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.19", "verse_number": 19, "en": "The term, horses, signifieth dominions in the lower heavens.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.20", "verse_number": 20, "en": "The term, cow, usually meaneth adaptability to the creative period. In the original Panic, cow meant receptivity, as in English a term of dollars signifieth the extent of a man's possessions, although he may have only lands and houses. So horses in the Yi-ha had no reference to the animal horse, nor had cow any reference to the animal cow. But in the lapse of ages, these figures received an earthly interpretation.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.21", "verse_number": 21, "en": "Hukairya, a kingdom in etherea.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.22", "verse_number": 22, "en": "The Lords of atmospherea ministering through the temples and oracles to mortals of the Hyan period, and embraced in Mithra inspiration, were as followeth: Maidhyozaremaya, Moidhyosheema, Paitis-hahaya, Ayathrecma, Maidhyairya, Hamacpathmoedaya, the Holy Lordess, the Gatha-Ahunavaiti, Yacna-Haptan-haiti, the Goddess Mother, Gatha-Ustavaiti, her Holy Sister, Goddess Gatha-Cpenta-Mainyu, her Holy Daughter, Goddess Gatha-Vohu-Khsha-thra, the Lord of Measure, Airyama, Fshnsha-manthea, Hadhaokhta, Creator, Ever Present Spirit in all places, Ruler over all else and Dispenser.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.23", "verse_number": 23, "en": "(For the origin of the words refer to the cyclean period of Ahura.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.24", "verse_number": 24, "en": "Mazdayacniaus, Faithists in the Great Spirit. Opposite to idolaters. The Haptans did not worship the Lords nor Gods, but revered them as exalted spirits sent from etherea, to minister to mortals, both through the temples and oracles, and in the family spirit circle, even as Christians of this day believe that Christ ministereth. With the Haptans, however, Mithra occupied the position that Christ doth in men's eyes, and the Lords and Lordesses, and Gods and Goddesses, were under Him, alternately with Yima.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": [], "images": [{"src": "/plates/edition1882/image208.jpg", "edition": "1882"}]}, {"id": "cosmology.11.25", "verse_number": 25, "en": "The traveling hosts from heaven were as followeth: Havanana, Atarevakhsha, Fraberata, Abert, Agnata, Racthwiskare and Craoshavareza.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.26", "verse_number": 26, "en": "Haoma, juice, milk, that which is received. As milk nurtureth the corporeal man, so haoma feedeth the spirit, i.e., spirit nutrition.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.27", "verse_number": 27, "en": "After the invocation to Ahura'Mazda, the Creator, the All Brilliant, the All Majestic, the All Greatest, Best and Most Beautiful, then the following Gods are invited, to wit:", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.28", "verse_number": 28, "en": "Vohu-mano, who is the voice and engraved word. (In the back period the Scriptures were taught orally, man to man, repeating over and over the same texts, until the whole three thousand Holy and Most Sacred Verses were learned. The position of the Most Holy Lord, Vohu, was to be present in spirit and person or through His representative spirit underlings, and see to it that there were no innovations in the original text, and to assist the learner to remember the words. Vohu, i.e., ga-mo, signifieth voice. Mano, i.e., c'fome, signifieth word in the Yi-ha language.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.29", "verse_number": 29, "en": "Ashavahista (properly Ashaohavah), the God of self-subduing. To teach and assist man to put aside selfish desires.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.30", "verse_number": 30, "en": "Ksha-thra-vairya (Ka-sha-thrag-o-mak), the Most Holy Teacher and Bracer-up of Faith.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": [], "images": [{"src": "/plates/edition1882/image185.jpg", "edition": "1882"}]}, {"id": "cosmology.11.31", "verse_number": 31, "en": "Cpenta-armaiti (originally feminine), Goddess and God, Adapter. That which within man maketh him compatible to others. (Yi-ha.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.32", "verse_number": 32, "en": "Haurvat, home. He who made spiritual homes in heaven for mortals, and brought the Kingdom of the Father to men's souls. (Yi-ha.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.33", "verse_number": 33, "en": "Amareth, forgiveness. Anh-air-that (Yi-ha). To make one's self child-like and willing to learn. To make one's soul like a sieve, that it will not hold anger.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.34", "verse_number": 34, "en": "Havani, the Bearer of souls to the second heaven. The Most Holy Lord of Transit. (Yi-ha.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.35", "verse_number": 35, "en": "The emissaries under these, the Most Holy Lords, active on earth, were called, Cavanhi-Vicya. Twenty-eight of these helps and Lords appointed ministered in each and all the temples of Baragas (Upper Tibet), and during the religious ceremonies of mortals were often seen participating near the altars.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.36", "verse_number": 36, "en": "Ushi-darena, a mountain above which the Most Holy Lords held their spirit Congress for over three hundred years! In this Congress, Zantuma was president. It was through this Spirit Congress that Mithra, the Savior of the Haptans of that day, promulgated the doctrine that Ahura'Mazda was not Creator, but merely the star (Iaza) Jupiter and that he, Mithra, was the Chief and Highest of all personages, either on earth or in heaven. This was the beginning of a war in heaven betwixt Yima and Mithra.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.37", "verse_number": 37, "en": "Arbury, the Father's Kingdom, literally around about all worlds. Alburj (Yi'ha), Gaisi mortal (or earthly things), became confounded with heavenly, for the migration of the people on earth confounded the stratagems of Mithra, the Savior.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.38", "verse_number": 38, "en": "Gah, the change of the watch of the Gods. A prefix to the names of Gods. Gah, true, a fact (Gau). The times of day and evening and night.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.39", "verse_number": 39, "en": "Fradat-vira, God of numeration of mortals, interest in progeny.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.40", "verse_number": 40, "en": "Nairy-Canha, God of messengers. All spirits coming from Mithra's throne in atmospherea as messengers were under the command of Mairya-canha. Of these there were thirty-three messengers-in-chief, and they held offices for one year, when they were replaced by new appointments. When the time of changing watch came, they gave to mortals ten days for feast, five days in honor of the ex-messengers, and five days in honor of the new messengers. It was customary to have thirty vases or dishes in the temples, adapted to as many varieties of food, and each and all of these were also named after the name of the spirit messengers.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.41", "verse_number": 41, "en": "Zaothra, holy water, also God of sprinkling. When the worshipers were assembled they were frequently sprinkled with water by the spirits.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": [], "images": [{"src": "/plates/edition1882/image210.jpg", "edition": "1882"}]}, {"id": "cosmology.11.42", "verse_number": 42, "en": "Zaota, a priest, through whom the Gods can cause rain to fall.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.43", "verse_number": 43, "en": "Barecme, literally, spirit light. Some were baptized by water, and some by fire.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.44", "verse_number": 44, "en": "Moon Gods, four in number, were usually changed once in seven years. But when Mithra proclaimed himself God and Savior of (the Indian) heaven, he allotted the moon Gods a hundred years each. But the chief of these remained in office for four hundred years. This was:", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.45", "verse_number": 45, "en": "Arstat, chief moon God of that period, and a friend to the God Craosha. Mortals, however, continued to keep holy one day in seven, in honor of the moon's changes, which were accredited to the change of watch of the Gods. In the original worship mortals were taught to wish hither, with love and praise, the four reigning Gods. But they were so full of devotion that on the seventh day they remembered many of the ex-Gods also, sometimes a hundred or more, all of whom they wished hither with love and praise. Many of those Gods (spirits), taking advantage of the devotion of mortals, established themselves in petty kingdoms in atmospherea belonging to Vind'yu, and thus aggrandized themselves, making slaves of spirits newborn into heaven.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.46", "verse_number": 46, "en": "Beside the week-day festivals, the Gods of Mithra, through the priests and magicians, established monthly festivals, on which occasion the Gods were honored with new prayers, and all these Lords and Gods of the lower heavens, were awarded by Mithra with petty kingdoms in atmospherea belonging to Vind'yu. Usually, each mortal city was allotted to the keeping of one of these spirit Lords or Gods, and the guardian spirits of mortals within that city were subjects to such chief Lord or God. The places of meeting of these spirits were in temples of worship. And all of the foregoing Lords, Saviors and Gods professed to give the revelations of Zarathustra, who had ceased in men's eyes to be a man, but a principle of Truth descended from Ahura'Mazda, Creator.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.47", "verse_number": 47, "en": "Myazda or Dracona,, feast of sacrament. Rice or other meal made into cakes and ate in remembrance of the VOW TO PURIFY MYSELF.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.48", "verse_number": 48, "en": "Haoma, in the latter sacrament of the Vede, was saluted as heaven's perfect type of corporeal beauty and cleanliness.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": [], "images": [{"src": "/plates/edition1882/image172.jpg", "edition": "1882"}]}, {"id": "cosmology.11.49", "verse_number": 49, "en": "Yima, a self-made God in after times, who announced himself, Son of Vivanho.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.50", "verse_number": 50, "en": "Cruvara, serpent with four legs. This was the lizard species, and in the time of Yi-ha they were sufficiently large to eat twelve full-grown men at a meal. They were of a dark green color, and fifty paces in length.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": [], "images": [{"src": "/plates/edition1882/image174.jpg", "edition": "1882"}, {"src": "/plates/edition1882/image206.jpg", "edition": "1882"}]}, {"id": "cosmology.11.51", "verse_number": 51, "en": "Gaccus, a giant who contrived traps to destroy the great serpents, the Cruvaras.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.52", "verse_number": 52, "en": "Asha, oratory. Power of reciting with effect.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.53", "verse_number": 53, "en": "Fravashi, pure spirits of the Faithist order, i.e., spirits who are not bound to idols, Gods, nor Saviors, but having faith in Ahura'Mazda, the Creator.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.54", "verse_number": 54, "en": "The opposers in heaven to the Fravashi were: The Daeva, Pairika, Cathra, Kaoza and the Karapana.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": [], "images": [{"src": "/plates/edition1882/image176.jpg", "edition": "1882"}, {"src": "/plates/edition1882/image220.jpg", "edition": "1882"}]}, {"id": "cosmology.11.55", "verse_number": 55, "en": "Verethragha, a God in heaven who labored for the Fravashi and against their opposers.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.56", "verse_number": 56, "en": "The following plateaux in the lower heavens belong to Vind'yu are often described as mountains, and in later days earth-mountains have had corresponding names given to them. That is to say: Mount Tshidarena, Haraiti, Zereaho, Ushidhao, Ushidarena, Erezifya, Fraorepa, Arezura, Bumza, Eezisho, Arana, Bazana, Vapa, Iskata, Kancotofedhra, Hamankuna, Vagna, Fravanku, Vidwana, Aezaka, Maenaka, Vakhedhrakae, Acaya, Tudhockoe, Ishvkoe,Asnavao, Draoshisvao, Cairivao, Nanhusmao, Kakahyu, Anterekanha, Cichindavaoo, Ahuna, Racmana, Asha-ctimbana, Urunyvovaidhae, Ushaoma, Usta-garenas, Cyamaka, Vafrayo, Voouusha, Jatara, Adhutavao, Cpitavarenao, Cpentodata, Kadrva-acpa, Kaoirica, Taera, Barocrayana, Barana, Frapavao, Udrya and Kaevao. To each and every one of the foregoing plateaux in atmospherea was assigned a God or a Lord, and these had direct superintendence over the affairs of mortals vertically under them. (Vede.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": [], "images": [{"src": "/plates/edition1882/image198.jpg", "edition": "1882"}]}, {"id": "cosmology.11.57", "verse_number": 57, "en": "Kanculoo, a running (es) sea in atmospherea, dividing the foregoing plateaux.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.58", "verse_number": 58, "en": "Caoshyanc, God of the (es) sea, Kanculoo, supposed by people in darkness to be the God whose coming would be the Second coming, or manifestation of Ahura'Mazda on earth. It was believed that with his second coming all sin on earth would be put down, and all things revealed. Caoshyanc, a Savior; to sin not. (Anar.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": [], "images": [{"src": "/plates/edition1882/image178.jpg", "edition": "1882"}]}, {"id": "cosmology.11.59", "verse_number": 59, "en": "Franrava, God of the Turanians, the opposers of Faithists. He who inspired the Turanians to war and to deeds of cruelty.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.60", "verse_number": 60, "en": "Parodars, an angel, a bird, a picture, or as one looking in a mirror sees himself. That which he sees reflected is parodars. Thus, when a pure man dieth, his soul for three days remaineth near the head of his corporeal body, reciting prayers and anthems, but on the fourth day he waketh to his condition and riseth and goeth forth. The first living creature he seeth is parodars (a female); a flying person of great beauty. He saluteth her, inquiring who she is. She answereth him, saying: I am thine own soul and good thoughts. I am the law thou hast builded up on the corporeal earth. Behold me, I am thyself, and now thou seest thine own self. I am most beautiful, because thy earth-thoughts were beautiful. I am pure because thy earth-thoughts were pure. Put away thyself and come thou and inhabit myself. I am the part that can ascend to nirvana, the second heaven; thou art the part that dwelleth as a druj, a bound spirit. On the fifth day the pure man putteth away self and inhabiteth the parodars, and so ascendeth and becometh a Lord in heaven. (Foivitat.)", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.61", "verse_number": 61, "en": "Foivitat saith: If an evil man die, his soul remaineth at his feet three days. On the fourth it goeth abroad; but because of its clouds, it beholdeth not parodars, the smothered bird, but it goeth into places that stink the nose, to the places that deafen the ears, to the places that blind the eyes, and, like a druj, can not speak truth, can not find love, can not learn. The soul of such a man becometh the inhabitant of foul houses and of battle-fields where madness liveth on madness, and evil spirits can not depart.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.62", "verse_number": 62, "en": "Kava-viscacpa, a councilor and friend of Zarathustra, a high, Holy Lord and Giver of Truth.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.63", "verse_number": 63, "en": "Hura, a one-time man.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.64", "verse_number": 64, "en": "Hura the entity, HAPPINESS. In all the Avanischor system, which descended to the Haptans and afterward to the Hijans, and finally to the Vedes of the Upper Tibet, happiness is called an entity; so is unhappiness; so is faith; so is unbelief; and they are likened to seeds planted, which grow by nurture, according to the behavior of mortals, into great trees. If, therefore, a man strive for Hura (happiness), it will grow in him, and not until he so striveth. And likewise of the other entities.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.65", "verse_number": 65, "en": "Kam, the air, the unseen atmosphere, or Kam-qactra, the high air, etherea, beyond the earth's atmosphere; that part of the firmament beyond the earth and earth's atmosphere which hath laws of its own. Etherea, the higher abode for exalted spirits who have risen above corporeal laws. As Anra'mainyus (evil), in atmospherea, looketh down-ward to the earth to evil, so Cpenta'mainyus (good), of etherea, looketh upward to good.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "Khahnaothra, an Ahurian of the Zarathustrian period.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "Ardvi-cara, an Ahurian of the Zarathustrian period.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "Rashnu, a Fragapattician of the Yi-ha period.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "Haha-Naepta (Goddess) of the host of Fragapatti, of the Theantiyi period. By the Ayustrians, Gathas meant Gods.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "Iaya-Haptanhaiti, special to Haptan, of the Hi-ga period.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "Ctatoa-Zacnya (Goe-howjhi), an Ahurian of the Fragapatti period.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "Naotara, of Aphian period, an instructor, who gave many sciences to mortals. These sciences and religious ceremonies were afterward called his sons, and they are now called Fravashis. In addition to the sciences this Lord God, through oracles and otherwise, revealed two hundred and seventy kingdoms in the lower heavens, the most important of which are: Zairi-vairi, Yukhata-vairi, Crisookhshau, Kerecaokhshan, Vyarez, Vanara, Bujicravo, Berejzarsti, Tizhyarsti, Perethwarsu, Vezhyarsti, Naptva, Vazhacpa, Habacpa, Victavaru and Frans-hanm-vareta. All of these divisions, including the two hundred kingdoms, had spirits-in-chief (Lord Gods) to each and every one who took up stations in the temples of worship on earth, and employed thousands of spirit servants, whom they allotted to the different mortals who came thither to worship, to be their guides and guardians, day and night. Through the prophets and high priests in the midnight worship, and also at dawn in the morning, these spirits appeared in tangible forms, taking part in the ceremonies.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "Thrita, God of healing, the founder of a mortal race to whom he revealed the secret remedies for all diseases. He enjoined that the remedies should only be revealed from father to son on the death-bed, and when the father thus revealed, he himself lost all power to heal.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "Hiac-kaus, Lord of the Seal of Heaven. He bestowed the power of Ahura'Mazda on mortals, enabling the prayers of the living to redeem from torments the spirits of their forefathers.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "Takma-wrupa, cunning. The entity cunning. Like a fox, whose strength lieth in cunning. Like the wind. Like the air; subtle, appearing mild; appearing nothing, yet full of deceptive poison. By takma-urupa the successful man is successful. He bindeth apparent goodness in his face, but his golden foot-stool is cunning, the air with the golden tuft.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "Ashi-vanuhi, Goddess of dress. She was fourth Airon under Mithra. The duties of her inspiration to mortal women were to clothe themselves and to decorate themselves with gold and silver ornaments. She had twelve hundred Goddesses under her, and they were allotted one day in each month to speak and teach through the magicians and oracles and prophets and high priests. Some of these spirits spoke through the seers by entrancement, and some wrote on the sand-table. Prior to this period Iranian women seldom wore clothes.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "The Voice was, The Great Spirit, The I am.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "Zarathustra, being all pure, taught that to be a Faithist in the Voice, as it cometh to the pure, was the highest that man could attain.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.3", "verse_number": 3, "en": "Zarathustra, being all pure, taught that to build up one's own faith in the I AM would produce the highest happiness.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.4", "verse_number": 4, "en": "Zarathustra, being all pure, taught that each self must learn to build up itself in love and wisdom, and after them, power, trusting in the I AM.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.5", "verse_number": 5, "en": "As the Voice came to Zarathustra, the all pure, Zarathustra perceived that the I AM must have a name in order to be distinguished by men.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.6", "verse_number": 6, "en": "Zarathustra said, Or (Light) Mazd (entity of, i.e., light, per se), and he called the HIGHEST KNOWN, Or'mazd, being The Person, The All Master.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.7", "verse_number": 7, "en": "Or'mazd spake to Zarathustra, saying: Some have I created with desire to dance, some with desire to sing, some with desire to pray.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.8", "verse_number": 8, "en": "Some have I created with faith in men, some with faith in spirits, some with faith in Me only.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.9", "verse_number": 9, "en": "Let those who have faith in men, have faith in men; let those who have faith in spirits, have faith in spirits; let those who have faith in Me, have faith in Me. The last are Mine. What is Mine I will gather together.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.10", "verse_number": 10, "en": "Mine shall be a people by themselves, of themselves.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.11", "verse_number": 11, "en": "Or'mazd spake to Zarathustra, saying:", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.12", "verse_number": 12, "en": "Or'mazd spake to Zarathustra, saying: Take the ten suggestions: what are they?", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.13", "verse_number": 13, "en": "Zarathustra said: My flesh is not my own substance, but Or'mazd's. How then can I claim the ten suggestions? These, then, are not the Zarathustrian Law. They are the Or'mazdian Law. All things come from Him. All things are His.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.14", "verse_number": 14, "en": "Zarathustra went forth, preaching, and his followers were numbered by hundreds of thousands.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": [], "images": [{"src": "/plates/edition1882/image162.jpg", "edition": "1882"}, {"src": "/plates/edition1882/image164.jpg", "edition": "1882"}, {"src": "/plates/edition1882/image166.jpg", "edition": "1882"}, {"src": "/plates/edition1882/image168.jpg", "edition": "1882"}, {"src": "/plates/edition1882/image170.jpg", "edition": "1882"}, {"src": "/plates/edition1882/image214.jpg", "edition": "1882"}]}, {"id": "cosmology.11.15", "verse_number": 15, "en": "Zarathustra worked no miracles. He said miracles were the tricks of spirits and mortals. The highest of all good was to do good, and be good.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.16", "verse_number": 16, "en": "God gave not to the Zarathustrians as to Israel, to move amongst strangers, but to dwell amidst the heathen within their own countries. And the Zarathustrians never established kings of their own. Nevertheless they fell into constant persecution by the worshipers of Gods and Lords.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.17", "verse_number": 17, "en": "By the time of Brahma they were nearly destroyed. And again God raised them up and established them as a mighty people. But being non-resistants they were again nearly destroyed. And again God raised them up through Capilya; this was in the time of Moses.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.18", "verse_number": 18, "en": "And they prospered and became numerous in all of Vind'yu and Jaffeth. For four hundred years they were a great people.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": [], "images": [{"src": "/plates/edition1882/image202.jpg", "edition": "1882"}, {"src": "/plates/edition1882/image216.jpg", "edition": "1882"}, {"src": "/plates/edition1882/image218.jpg", "edition": "1882"}]}, {"id": "cosmology.11.19", "verse_number": 19, "en": "And about the time the Israelites made Saul their king, darkness came upon the Zarathustrians also.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.20", "verse_number": 20, "en": "The Lords and Saviors, through the oracles, inspired the kings and rulers to despoil the Zarathustrians. And for nine hundred years they were persecuted and tortured; and millions of them put to death. So that the name of Zarathustra was forgotten amongst men. And the great learning, and light and knowledge, of those nations, went out, to return not for a long season.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.21", "verse_number": 21, "en": "God said: Because they have persecuted and destroyed my chosen, I will turn my face away from them, and they shall go down in darkness. Behold, when they persecuted my chosen in the land of Egupt, I shut out the light from them, and they perished.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.22", "verse_number": 22, "en": "Let this be testimony unto all peoples, that whosoever divideth my people or despoileth them, shall also be divided and despoiled.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.23", "verse_number": 23, "en": "This is a law of Jehovih; whoso goeth away from Him shall not find Him; to turn from Him is to curse Him; to curse Him is to curse those that do it, and it shall be answered unto them.", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.1", "verse_number": 1, "en": "These are the words of Bon: Thou, O Jehovih! Who can fashion Thee with words, or show Thy immensity? Where stood Thy feet in the time of Creation or rested Thy hand!", "zh_hant": null, "zh_hans": null, "ja": null, "glossary_terms": [], "plate_ref": null, "notes": []}, {"id": "cosmology.11.2", "verse_number": 2, "en": "Thou Present and Afar! Thou Who art older than time, O Jehovih!
